### [66] [Hardware-accelerated Aggregation: Unification and Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10030)
*Alireza Shateri,Hongshi Tan,Michael Ng,Bingsheng He,Qizhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在统一硬件加速框架下实现了聚合操作，并在CPU、GPU和FPGA三种平台上对比评估了性能与能耗，探索了硬件加速在数据分析中的权衡与优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域专用硬件的高效性引发了在数据分析系统中采用加速器的浓厚兴趣。聚合作为数据分析的关键操作，其硬件加速方案值得深入研究，且GPU与FPGA在云数据中心已广泛部署。

Method: 采用统一的硬件加速框架实现聚合操作，以编程便捷性和可移植性为权衡，并在其基础上进一步开发硬件特定的优化策略。

Result: 在CPU、GPU和FPGA三种近期计算硬件平台上进行了系统性评估，从设备端和端到端处理两个维度，全面度量了性能和能耗指标。

Conclusion: 通过对比不同硬件平台的加速效果，揭示了硬件加速解决方案在数据分析聚合操作中的性能与能耗权衡，为选择合适加速方案提供了实证依据。

Abstract: The high efficiency of domain-specific hardware has sparked substantial interest in adopting accelerators in data analytics systems. Among many choices, GPUs and FPGAs thrived as two popular solutions due to their prevalent deployments in cloud data centers. This paper investigates hardware acceleration solutions for aggregation, a critical data analytics operation. Specifically, we implement aggregation with a unified hardware acceleration framework, which trades efficiency for ease of programming and portability, and then further develop hardware-specific optimizations. We evaluate these solutions on three recent computing hardware platforms: a CPU, a GPU, and an FPGA, with metrics that cover both the performance and energy consumption of on-device and end-to-end processing.

</details>


### [67] [RATrain: A Resource-Aware Training Runtime for Large Language Models on Bandwidth-Constrained Heterogeneous Supercomputing Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10415)
*Yao Lu,Shiqing Ma,Zhongzhi Luan,Gen Li,Jiaxing Qi,Bin Han,Hailong Yang,Depei Qian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了RATrain，一种面向带宽受限异构超算平台的资源感知大语言模型训练运行时，在MT-3000平台上相比GPU风格训练策略实现了最高1.35倍端到端加速，在1024计算集群上达到97.0%的扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU导向训练运行时依赖高带宽设备内存、快速互连和成熟的集合通信库，难以直接适配具有显式内存层次结构、有限可用DDR容量和受限集群间通信能力的MT-3000异构超算平台。

Method: RATrain将标准非交错1F1B训练建模为训练状态生命周期调度问题，在层级和阶段局部粒度上调度梯度同步、参数更新、参数视图预取和激活恢复。系统包含MT-3000感知执行后端（用于高效FP16 GEMM、注意力反向传播和显式数据移动）以及资源感知规划器（在每计算集群20GB可用DDR约束下选择可行训练配置）。

Result: 在真实MT-3000平台上使用LLaMA-2-7B、Baichuan2-13B、Qwen2.5-32B和LLaMA-2-70B配置评估，RATrain相比适配MT-3000的GPU风格训练策略实现最高1.35倍端到端加速。对于LLaMA-2-7B，系统扩展至1024计算集群，达到112,790.55 tokens/s吞吐量，扩展效率为97.0%。10.28亿token正确性验证显示RATrain保持了语义等价Baseline-1F1B运行的损失轨迹，最大相对损失偏差为0.081%。

Conclusion: RATrain成功解决了带宽受限异构超算平台上的大语言模型训练挑战，通过资源感知的训练状态生命周期调度和平台优化的执行后端，在保持训练正确性的同时显著提升了训练性能和扩展效率。

Abstract: Production heterogeneous supercomputing platforms are increasingly used to host large language model (LLM) training workloads. However, existing GPU-oriented training runtimes typically rely on high-bandwidth device memory, fast interconnects, and mature collective communication libraries, making them difficult to directly adapt to MT-3000, a platform with an explicit memory hierarchy, limited usable DDR capacity, and constrained inter-cluster communication. This paper presents RATrain, a resource-aware training runtime for dense LLMs on bandwidth-constrained heterogeneous supercomputing platforms. RATrain formulates standard non-interleaved 1F1B training as a training-state lifecycle scheduling problem, and schedules gradient synchronization, parameter update, parameter-view prefetching, and activation recovery at layer-level and stage-local granularity. RATrain further combines an MT-3000-aware execution backend for efficient and predictable FP16 GEMM, Attention Backward, and explicit data movement with a resource-aware planner that selects feasible training configurations under the 20GB usable-DDR constraint per compute cluster. We implement RATrain on a real MT-3000 platform and evaluate it using LLaMA-2-7B, Baichuan2-13B, Qwen2.5-32B, and LLaMA-2-70B configurations. Results show that RATrain achieves up to 1.35$\times$ end-to-end speedup over MT-3000-adapted GPU-style training strategies. For LLaMA-2-7B, RATrain scales to 1024 compute clusters, reaches 112,790.55 tokens/s, and achieves 97.0\% scaling efficiency. A further 1.028B-token correctness run shows that RATrain preserves the loss trajectory of a semantically equivalent Baseline-1F1B run, with a maximum relative loss deviation of 0.081\%.

</details>


### [68] [ASTRA-sim 3.0: Next-Level Distributed Machine Learning Simulations via High-Fidelity GPU and Infrastructure Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10440)
*William Won,Jinsun Yoo,Tuan Ta,Moumita Dey,Andy Balogh,Pradosh Datta,Furkan Eris,Conor Green,Winston Liu,Changhai Man,Kingshuk Mandal,Amos Rai,Vinay Ramakrishnaiah,Ruchi Shah,David Sidler,Harsh Sikhwal,Hanjiang Wu,Tushar Krishna,Bradford M. Beckmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式机器学习推理场景下的延迟敏感集合通信建模需求，增强了开源模拟器ASTRA-sim，通过引入细粒度GPU执行模型和标准化的基础设施表示InfraGraph，实现了高保真与可扩展性平衡的模拟，为集合算法、网络需求和GPU架构的设计空间探索提供了新机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式机器学习推理成为关键应用场景，对延迟敏感的集合通信进行高保真建模变得至关重要。现有模拟器难以同时捕获设备架构和控制/数据路径细节，且缺乏统一的基础设施表示，限制了设计空间探索能力。

Method: 提出ASTRA-sim 3.0，在缓存行粒度的load-store级别进行模拟，结合详细的GPU执行模型以平衡可扩展性与保真度；引入InfraGraph标准表示法，详细捕获分布式ML网络基础设施。

Result: 基于增强后的ASTRA-sim 3.0，展示了在优化集合算法设计、网络需求分析及GPU架构探索等方面的设计空间探索案例，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过高保真、细粒度模拟与标准化基础设施表示的结合，ASTRA-sim 3.0为分布式机器学习系统的性能优化和架构设计提供了更精准的模拟工具和更广阔的探索空间。

Abstract: Distributed machine learning (ML) is a key paradigm for today's large-scale artificial intelligence applications. As model inference arises as an important use case, faithful modeling of latency-sensitive collective communication has never been more important. Capturing the device architecture and modeling control and data paths at high fidelity is therefore a necessity today. Having a common, detailed representation for distributed ML infrastructure is also crucial. We revisit the promising open-source, community-driven simulator: ASTRA-sim. In this work, we identify limitations of the current ASTRA-sim simulator and augment it with new features. To this end, we enable fine-grained, high-fidelity simulation with a standardized infrastructure representation, opening new design space exploration opportunities. We propose the simulation at cache-line-sized load-store granularity, with a detailed graphics processing unit (GPU) execution model, to balance simulation scalability and fidelity. We also introduce InfraGraph, a standardized representation to capture distributed ML network infrastructure in detail. Using the updated ASTRA-sim 3.0 simulator, we showcase interesting design space explorations for designing optimized collective algorithms, network requirements, and GPU architectures.

</details>


### [69] [Achieving Cloud-Grade SLOs for Local Mixture-of-Experts Inference through CPU-GPU Hybrid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10493)
*Wenxin Wang,Yule Hou,Yu Ji,Peng Qu,Youhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种CPU-GPU混合系统，通过流式加载预填充、分布式专家并行、预填充-解码分离、FP8优化内核和细粒度并行等技术，在消费级硬件上实现大MoE模型的云端级服务质量，支持32K-45K提示词在30秒内完成预填充，解码速度达28 tokens/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署大型MoE模型存在四个关键问题：依赖容量缩减模型（量化、蒸馏、重路由）、长预填充无法满足30秒TTFT要求、解码吞吐量低于20 tokens/s、混合工作负载下并发性能差。这些限制导致本地部署无法达到云端级服务质量。

Method: 提出CPU-GPU混合系统，包含五个核心创新：(1)流式加载预填充(SLP)，将预填充吞吐量提升至1,200 tokens/s；(2)分布式SLP(DSLP)配合SmallEP专家并行，在两块RTX 5090上达1,800 tokens/s；(3)节点内预填充-解码分离，采用零拷贝共享权重和双批次attention-MoE重叠机制；(4)AVX-512优化的FP8 GEMV内核，实现原生CPU FP8推理，延迟降低4-5倍；(5)细粒度CPU并行化。

Result: 在双插槽消费级CPU和消费级GPU上实现：支持32K提示词在30秒内完成预填充(SLP)，分布式配置支持45K提示词；并发性能下延迟增加低于15%，吞吐量提升50%；INT4 DeepSeek-V3达28 tokens/s，完整FP8 V3达21.5 tokens/s；CPU FP8推理延迟降低4-5倍。系统在消费级平台上为旗舰MoE模型提供云端级QoS。

Conclusion: 该系统在消费级CPU-GPU平台上实现了大型MoE模型的云端级服务质量，无需数据中心基础设施即可提供高质量、成本效益的访问，支持完整原始精度推理而非缩减模型，重塑了本地部署的可能性。

Abstract: Local deployment of large Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models falls short of the service quality achieved in cloud-scale environments, even under low-concurrency workloads. We identify four key gaps in local MoE inference: reliance on capacity-reduced models (quantized, distilled, rerouted), inability to meet 30-second TTFT for long prefills (more than 12K), sub-baseline decode throughput (under 20 tokens/s), and poor concurrency under mixed prefill-decode and batched decode workloads. We present a CPU-GPU hybrid system that achieves cloud-level SLOs on dual-socket commodity CPUs and consumer GPUs by (1) stream-loading prefill (SLP), boosting prefill throughput to 1,200 tokens/s and enabling 32K prompts within 30 seconds; (2) distributed SLP (DSLP) with SmallEP expert parallelism, reaching 1,800 tokens/s and 45K prompts in 30 seconds on two RTX 5090s; (3) intra-node prefill-decode disaggregation with zero-copy shared weights and a dual-batch attention-MoE overlap scheme, sustaining concurrency with under 15 percent latency increase and 50 percent throughput gains; (4) an AVX-512-optimized FP8 GEMV kernel, enabling native CPU FP8 inference while delivering 4-5x lower CPU latency; and (5) fine-grained CPU parallelism that attains 28 tokens/s on INT4 DeepSeek-V3 and 21.5 tokens/s on intact FP8 V3. Evaluations show our system delivers cloud-level QoS for flagship MoE models on consumer CPU-GPU platforms, reshaping local deployment with intact, original-precision inference and enabling high-quality, cost-effective access without datacenter infrastructure.

</details>


### [70] [Generalizing LCL Complexity Gaps to Unbounded Degree via Monadic Second-Order Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10693)
*Chiara Piombi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将LCL问题推广到无界度图，提出了LPMSO问题类，并证明了在无界度根树上，经典的复杂度间隙结论仍然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LCL问题分类结果仅适用于有界度图，而许多重要问题需要在无界度图上定义，这限制了现有分类结果的适用范围。

Method: 使用Presburger一元二阶（PMSO）公式将LCL问题推广到无界度图，定义了Local PMSO（LPMSO）问题类，即正确解由PMSO公式有限描述且可被LOCAL算法在常数时间内局部验证的问题。

Result: 在无界度根树上，LPMSO问题类同样存在ω(log n)-n^o(1)和ω(n^{1/(k+1)})-o(n^{1/k})的复杂度间隙，且间隙的可判定性保持成立。

Conclusion: 成功将LCL问题的经典复杂度分类结果扩展到了无界度图上的LPMSO问题类，突破了原有理论的有界度限制。

Abstract: The last decade of research on the LOCAL model has seen tremendous progress in understanding locally checkable labeling (LCL) problems, culminating in an almost complete classification of the possible complexities LCL problems can exhibit. In particular, on undirected trees, Chang and Pettie showed that there is no LCL problem with complexity between $ω(\log n)$ and $n^{o(1)}$ and Chang showed that, for every positive integer $k$, there is no LCL problem with complexity between $ω(n^{1/(k+1)})$ and $o(n^{1/k})$; additionally, which side of each gap a problem is found on is decidable.
  While the class of LCL problems - which, roughly speaking, consists of problems for which the correctness of a solution can be described by a finite set of allowed node configurations, which in turn can be locally verified by a constant-time algorithm - includes many important problems, it has one major restriction: problems can be defined only on bounded degree graphs, which consequently restricts all the classification and gap results mentioned above.
  In this work, we propose a generalization of LCL problems to unbounded degree using Presburger monadic second-order (PMSO) formulas; more specifically, we consider what we call Local PMSO (LPMSO) problems, i.e., problems whose correct solutions are both finitely described by a PMSO formula and locally verifiable by a LOCAL algorithm in constant time - this class contains many of the important problems studied in the LOCAL model but defines them on unbounded degree graphs. As our main result we prove that, on unbounded degree rooted trees, the aforementioned $ω(\log n)$ - $n^{o(1)}$ and $ω(n^{1/(k+1)})$ - $o(n^{1/k})$ complexity gaps (and their decidability) extend to the class of LPMSO problems.

</details>


### [71] [Dynamic Software Updates using CRDTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10920)
*Seppe Wyns,Jim Bauwens,Elisa Gonzalez Boix*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CRDT的分布式应用动态更新方法，通过App CRDT复制代码并利用消息传递中间件处理向后不兼容的更新，实现了在AmbientTalk和LuAT中的跨语言通用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式应用程序的动态软件更新面临一致性和兼容性挑战，特别是在存在向后不兼容更新的情况下。研究者希望利用CRDT的无冲突复制特性来解决分布式环境中的代码更新同步问题。

Method: 提出了一种新的App CRDT数据结构，用于存储带有语义版本的应用程序代码，语义版本定义了代码更新的全序关系。该结构配合API兼容的消息传递中间件工作，使应用程序能够在部分组件已更新的情况下继续运行。研究者在AmbientTalk和LuAT两个编程语言/框架中实现了该方案。

Result: 成功在AmbientTalk和LuAT中实现了基于CRDT的代码复制方法。该方法可以集成到现有的AmbientTalk应用程序中，仅需最少的修改。证明了使用CRDT复制代码的方法可以推广到其他编程语言。

Conclusion: 使用CRDT进行动态软件更新是可行的，该方法能够处理向后不兼容的软件更新，具有良好的跨语言通用性，为分布式应用的动态更新提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates how Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) can be used for dynamic software updates of distributed applications. We propose to model application updates as a new App CRDT that stores the application code associated with a semantic version, which defines a total order of the code updates. The App CRDT works with an API-compatible message delivery middleware, which allows applications to continue working with partially updated components in the face of backwards-incompatible software updates. We implemented our approach in AmbientTalk, an ambient-oriented programming language designed for distributed systems. We show how this CRDT can be integrated with existing AmbientTalk applications, requiring minimal changes. We also implemented our approach in LuAT, an ambient-oriented programming framework for Lua. This shows that our approach of using CRDTs to replicate code can be generalised to other programming languages.

</details>


### [72] [FairWave : A Fairness-Aware Asynchronous DAG-BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10982)
*Syariful Mujaddiq*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FairWave是一种双通道DAG-BFT协议，通过将锚点选择与奖励分配解耦，有效解决了异步BFT与PoS结合时的三难困境，显著降低了中心化程度（基尼系数从0.488降至0.149），并提供了抗Sybil攻击和公平性的形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DAG-BFT方法（如Narwhal+Tusk、Bullshark和Mysticeti）将liveness优先于基于权益选择的公平性影响，导致持续的纵向中心化问题。将异步拜占庭容错（BFT）共识与权益证明（PoS）结合时，会在Sybil抗性、奖励分配公平性和防止持久财阀统治之间产生三难困境。

Method: FairWave采用双通道DAG BFT架构：选择通道以权益超线性方式运行，保证所有分裂因子K>1时Sybil增益<1；奖励通道以亚线性方式运行，使用平方根权益归一化来缓解富者愈富的动态。最终确定的DAG结构提供确定性的正常运行时间和延迟因子，允许诚实验证者就操作质量达成一致而无需外部预言机。为避免选择结果与选择权重之间的循环依赖，声誉以滞后形式使用：epoch e的活动值等于前一epoch的最终值。

Result: 通过约550,000次蒙特卡洛轮次的九项实证分析验证，FairWave实现了：基尼系数0.149（对比纯PoS的0.488），在50,000个epoch中HHI单调从0.039降至0.021，最优对抗者Sybil分裂K*=1，在±25%输入扰动下成功率变异系数为5.2%。安全性是2f+1强支持提交规则的形式化结果，对f<n/3无条件成立。实证差异表现为单调连续的liveness退化曲线，从b=0.20时99.6%提交率降至理论边界b=1/3时71.1%，没有视图更改驱动的领导者BFT特征的不连续悬崖。

Conclusion: FairWave成功解决了异步BFT与PoS结合的三难困境，通过双通道设计实现了Sybil抗性、奖励公平性和防止财阀统治的平衡，为去中心化系统提供了更公平的共识机制，避免了现有方法中的持久中心化问题。

Abstract: Combining asynchronous Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) consensus with Proof-of-Stake (PoS) creates a trilemma between Sybil resistance, reward distribution fairness, and protection against persistent plutocracy. Existing DAG-BFT approaches (Narwhal+Tusk, Bullshark, and Mysticeti) prioritize liveness over the fairness implications of stake-based selection, resulting in persistent longitudinal centralization.FairWave is a dual-channel DAG BFT protocol that separates anchor selection from reward distribution. The selection channel is super-linear in stake, guaranteeing Sybil gain < 1 for all split factors K > 1. The reward channel is sub-linear, using square-root stake normalization to mitigate rich-get-richer dynamics.The finalized DAG structure provides deterministic uptime and latency factors, allowing honest validators to agree on operational quality without any external oracle. To avoid circular dependency between selection outcomes and selection weights, reputation is used in a lagged form: the active value at epoch e equals the prior epoch's final value. We derive closed-form constraints for both channels and validate them through nine empirical analyses (approximately 550,000 Monte Carlo rounds) against eight baselines. FairWave achieves a Gini coefficient of 0.149 (vs. Pure-PoS's 0.488), a monotone HHI reduction from 0.039 to 0.021 over 50,000 epochs, an optimal-adversary Sybil split of K* = 1, and a success-rate coefficient of variation of 5.2% under +/-25% input perturbation. Safety (agreement and validity) is a formal consequence of the 2f+1 strong-support commit rule, holding unconditionally for f < n/3; the empirical differential is the monotone-continuous liveness-degradation curve, which decreases from 99.6% commit rate at b=0.20 to 71.1% at the theoretical bound b=1/3 without the discontinuous cliff characteristic of view-change-driven leader-BFT.

</details>


### [73] [A Neurosymbolic Prolog Skill for LLM-Driven Service Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11113)
*Jacopo Massa,Giuseppe Bisicchia,Patrizio Dazzi,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号方法，通过Prolog技能实现云边连续体中的约束感知服务放置，结合了语言模型的高层意图理解和形式化约束验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的云边连续体服务放置方法存在局限性：优化模型和启发式方法需要显式建模，而神经方法缺乏透明性和形式化保证。因此需要一种既能理解高层意图又能进行形式化约束验证的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于Prolog技能的神经符号方法。Prolog技能是一个可重用接口，用于模式约束的事实生成和查询。该方法使语言模型能够将放置意图结构化为符号事实、规则和查询，同时将验证和推理委托给Prolog系统，实现高层意图与形式化约束评估的桥接。

Result: 成功设计了一种神经符号替代方案，实现了可检查和策略感知的放置决策，能够在云边环境中处理延迟、局部性和策略要求等多种约束。

Conclusion: 该工作通过Prolog技能将神经方法与符号推理相结合，为云边连续体中的服务放置提供了一种透明、可检查且符合策略约束的新方法，解决了现有方法在透明性和形式化保证方面的不足。

Abstract: Service placement in the cloud-edge continuum requires assigning application components to heterogeneous resources under multiple constraints, including latency, locality, and policy requirements. Existing approaches rely on optimisation models or heuristics that require explicit modelling, while neural methods lack transparency and formal guarantees. This work proposes a neuro-symbolic alternative based on a Prolog skill, a reusable interface for schema-constrained fact generation and querying, for constraint-aware placement. The skill enables a language model to structure placement intent into symbolic facts, rules, and queries, while delegating validation and reasoning to Prolog. This design bridges high-level intent and formal constraint evaluation, enabling inspectable and policy-aware placement decisions in cloud-edge environments.

</details>


### [74] [Revisiting "Cooler is Better": ITD-Aware Per-CPU Thermal Optimization for Sustainable Data Center Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11163)
*Jason Crop,Hayden Moore,Sudeep Pasricha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 现代低电压CPU存在逆温度依赖性(ITD)，效率最优温度往往高于典型数据中心运行温度。通过ITD感知的热分组和入口温度调整，可在不影响性能和可靠性的情况下降低数据中心总能耗4-13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据中心能源需求接近电网级别限制，优化传统服务器基础设施对于可持续增长至关重要。长期以来的假设"越冷越好"（即较低的CPU温度降低功耗）对于现代低电压CPU并不完全适用，这促使研究者重新审视数据中心的热管理策略。

Method: 首次在生产级Intel Xeon CPU上实证表征逆温度依赖性(ITD)现象；在商业云数据中心平台（Amazon、Equinix）进行测量；实施ITD感知的CPU热分组和入口温度调整策略；进行案例研究验证方法效果。

Result: 效率最优温度具有CPU部件特定性，通常高于典型数据中心运行条件；约一半的现代高功耗CPU运行温度比其效率最优热点的低10°C；通过ITD感知的热管理方法可降低数据中心总能耗4-13%。

Conclusion: 研究推翻了"越冷越好"的传统假设，揭示了现代CPU效率峰值出现在中间温度点的非单调性能-瓦特曲线。通过优化热管理策略，数据中心运营商可以在不影响性能和可靠性的前提下显著提升设施级冷却效率和整体可持续性。

Abstract: As data center energy demand approaches grid-level constraints, optimizing conventional server infrastructure is essential for sustainable growth. The long-standing assumption that "cooler is better", i.e., lower CPU temperatures reduce power, does not fully hold for modern low-voltage CPUs, where inverse temperature dependence (ITD) drives higher supply voltages at lower temperatures. This creates a non-monotonic performance-per-watt curve where efficiency peaks at an intermediate thermal point. In this paper, for the first time, we empirically characterize ITD on production Intel Xeon CPUs and demonstrate that efficiency-optimal temperatures are CPU part-specific, and frequently higher than typical data center operating conditions. Measurements from commercial cloud data center platforms (Amazon, Equinix) reveal that approximately half of modern high-power CPUs operate about 10°C below their efficiency-optimal thermal point. By implementing ITD-aware thermal grouping of CPUs and inlet temperature adjustments, data center operators can optimize facility-level cooling and overall sustainability. Our case study shows that this approach can reduce total data center energy by 4-13% without sacrificing performance or reliability.

</details>


### [75] [Piper: A Programmable Distributed Training System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11169)
*Megan Frisella,Shubham Tiwari,Andy Ruan,Yi Pan,Parker Gustafson,Mat Jacob,Gilbert Bernstein,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Piper，一个用户可控的分布式训练系统，通过解耦策略与运行时实现，使用声明式注解和调度指令，在保持常见策略性能的同时，支持复杂并行策略的组合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模模型训练系统存在两难困境：一方面，生产环境依赖专家手动设计并行策略，难以适应新策略；另一方面，通用框架虽灵活但局限于固定策略集，难以集成最新优化技术。需要一种既能灵活表达新策略，又能高效执行的系统。

Method: Piper采用声明式方法，用户通过少量模型注解和调度指令定义分布式训练策略。每个指令对Piper的中间表示（IR）进行变换——IR是一个统一的全局训练DAG，表示所有计算和通信。系统基于IR编译生成每个设备的执行计划，并通过与策略无关的分布式运行时执行。核心是将策略声明与运行时实现解耦。

Result: 实验表明，Piper在常用策略（如ZeRO）上保持性能对等，同时在组合并行策略（如DeepSeek-V3的DualPipe）中，通过联合调度计算和通信，实现了额外的性能提升和内存效率改善。

Conclusion: Piper成功证明了将分布式训练策略与运行时实现解耦的可行性，既保持了系统的灵活性和可扩展性，又未牺牲执行效率，为集成和优化新的并行策略提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Large-scale model training increasingly relies on composing multiple parallelism strategies, such as data, pipeline, and expert parallelism, together with memory-saving optimizations like ZeRO. Deployed systems for foundation model pretraining often rely on human experts to manually design a high-level parallelism strategy then implement the corresponding low-level execution strategy, making it difficult to adapt the system to new strategies. Meanwhile, many general-purpose frameworks are more flexible but their implementations are still tied to a fixed set of common parallelism strategies, making it challenging to integrate state-of-the-art strategies.
  We present Piper, a user-controllable distributed training system that decouples the strategy from the runtime implementation. Piper allows users to declare a comprehensive distributed training strategy with a small set of model annotations and scheduling directives. Each directive applies a transformation on Piper's intermediate representation (IR), a unified global training DAG that represents all computation and communication. Using this IR, Piper compiles per-device execution plans and executes them with a distributed runtime agnostic to the strategy. We show that the combined system maintains performance parity on commonly available strategies such as ZeRO, while also enabling additional performance and memory efficiency gains through joint scheduling of compute and communication in composed parallelism strategies such as DeepSeek-V3's DualPipe.

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Mechanistic Analysis of Alignment Algorithms in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09850)
*Aarush Sinha,Ishan Garg,Veeraraju Elluru,Arth Singh,Kushal Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种主流偏好优化方法（PPO、DPO、SimPO、ORPO、GRPO、KTO）进行了系统的机制分析，发现不同算法在模型内部会引发质性不同的表征变化：KTO和GRPO通过建设性特征共享增强线性可分性，DPO和ORPO则通过几何旋转导致特征退化，PPO和SimPO基本保持原有几何结构。这表明对齐是异质性干预，需要标准化的特征级审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对训练后对齐算法的评估主要将其视为黑箱，缺乏对其如何重塑语言模型内部计算机制的理解。这种不透明性限制了对安全性和可解释性的深入分析，因此需要系统性地研究不同偏好优化方法在模型内部引发的具体变化。

Method: 研究整合了三种分析技术：层级线性探测、稀疏自编码器和交叉编码器，对三种开源权重模型家族上的六种偏好优化方法进行分析，定位偏好表征并量化潜在空间中对齐引起的几何变换。

Result: 偏好信号一致性地集中在早中期或中后期层，但不同优化目标引发质性不同的表征转变：KTO和GRPO通过建设性特征共享和稀疏、高显著性招募增强线性可分性；DPO和ORPO通过非建设性几何旋转和特征衰减降低可分性；PPO和SimPO基本保持基线几何结构。这些变换表现出架构依赖的变异性。

Conclusion: 研究发现确立了对齐作为异质性干预的性质，证明了行为对齐并不意味着统一的内部重构。这为安全性和可解释性研究提出了标准化特征级审计的需求，并强调需要开发机制感知的优化目标。

Abstract: Post-training alignment algorithms are predominantly evaluated as black boxes, obscuring how they reshape language models' internal computations. We present a systematic mechanistic analysis of six preference-optimization methods: PPO, DPO, SimPO, ORPO, GRPO, and KTO across three open-weight model families. By integrating layer-wise linear probing, Sparse Autoencoders, and crosscoders, we localize preference representations and quantify alignment-induced geometric transformations in latent space. We find that preference signals consistently concentrate in early--mid or mid--late layers, but different objectives induce qualitatively distinct representational shifts. KTO and GRPO enhance linear separability through constructive feature sharing and sparse, high-salience recruitment. In contrast, DPO and ORPO degrade separability via non-constructive geometric rotation and feature attenuation, while PPO and SimPO largely preserve baseline geometry. These transformations exhibit architecture-dependent variability, demonstrating that behavioral alignment does not imply uniform internal restructuring. Our findings establish alignment as a heterogeneous intervention, motivate standardized feature-level auditing for safety and interpretability, and highlight the need for mechanism-aware optimization objectives.

</details>


### [77] [SynIB: Informational Bottleneck for Maximizing Synergy in Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09853)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Teodora Gagaleska,Thomas Strypsteen,Christos Chatzichristos,Matthew Blaschko,Maarten De Vos,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出协同信息瓶颈(SynIB)方法，通过优化训练目标直接捕获多模态协同信息，在合成XOR任务和真实基准测试上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习的核心目标是捕获协同信息（仅来自多模态联合使用的任务相关信息），但标准训练往往强调单模态或冗余信息，在需要跨模态推理的样本上表现不佳。现有方法多从架构层面改进，缺乏对训练目标本身的优化。

Method: 从信息论角度形式化多模态协同性，提出协同信息瓶颈(SynIB)目标函数。模型在预测所有模态时追求准确性，同时对任意模态被遮蔽时仍保持自信的情况进行惩罚。除标准任务损失外，模型执行逐模态遮蔽的前向传播，惩罚单模态线索依赖而非跨模态交互。

Result: 在合成XOR任务中标准训练无法恢复真实协同信息而SynIB成功；在五个真实基准测试（包括MultiBench情感任务、Hateful Memes和CREMA-D可控反讽扩展）上，SynIB在依赖协同信息的样本上准确率提升高达7.8%，整体准确率提升高达3.8%。

Conclusion: SynIB为多模态学习提供了一种有效的方法来直接捕获协同信息，证明了优化训练目标是架构改进的可行补充方向。该方法在需要跨模态推理的任务上具有显著的性能提升和实际应用价值。

Abstract: A central objective in multimodal learning is to capture synergy: task-relevant information that arises only from the joint use of multiple modalities, and is not available from any single modality alone. While most approaches operate at the architectural level through larger or more complex fusion models, we propose a complementary axis: shaping the training objective itself. Standard training often emphasizes unimodal or redundant information, falling short on examples that require cross-modal reasoning. We formalize multimodal synergy through information theory and introduce the Synergistic Information Bottleneck (SynIB), a scalable objective that targets synergy directly. To prioritize learning synergy, SynIB motivates the model to predict accurately from all modalities while penalizing confidence when information from any modality is withheld. Alongside the standard task loss, the model runs forward passes with one modality masked at a time and is penalized for remaining confident, which would indicate reliance on unimodal cues rather than cross-modal interactions. We validate SynIB in two regimes. On synthetic XOR tasks where the ground-truth synergy is known by construction, standard training fails to recover it while SynIB does. On five real-world benchmarks, including three MultiBench affective tasks, Hateful Memes with CLIP-ViT and DeBERTa backbones, and a controllable irony extension of CREMA-D we introduce, SynIB improves accuracy on synergy-dependent examples by up to 7.8% and overall accuracy by up to 3.8%.

</details>


### [78] [TRAPS: Therapeutic Response Analysis via Pathway-informed Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09898)
*Sujoy Banik,Sayantan Chakraborty,Boishakhi Das Toma,Zainab Ghafoor,Ushashi Bhattacharjee,Koushik Howlader,Tirtho Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个通路引导治疗反应建模的统一基准，在五个癌症队列（2622名患者）上评估了三种生物学信息架构（BINN、GraphPath、PATH）。研究发现没有单一架构在所有任务中表现最优：PATH在靶向分子治疗预测中最佳，BINN在生存预测中最可靠，而所有模型在放疗预测中均无效。GraphPath在前列腺癌靶向治疗预测中达到0.92的AUROC，为整个基准测试最高分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症治疗规划需要同时在多个临床维度做出决策，包括是否接受靶向分子治疗、放疗以及患者是否可能存活超过六个月。现有的通路信息深度学习模型均在孤立环境中开发和测试，无法在不同架构间进行公平比较。因此需要建立一个统一的基准来公平评估不同模型在多任务临床预测中的表现。

Method: 构建首个通路引导治疗反应建模的统一基准，评估三种生物学信息架构（BINN、GraphPath、PATH）。使用来自癌症基因组图谱（TCGA）的五个癌症队列，涵盖2622名患者，采用Reactome通路活性评分进行编码。每个模型在相同的数据和评估条件下，联合训练所有三个临床结局（靶向分子治疗、放疗、生存预测），这是首次将通路结构化深度学习作为联合治疗和生存预测问题进行研究。

Result: 没有单一架构在所有任务中获胜：PATH在靶向分子治疗预测整体表现最佳，BINN在生存预测中最可靠，但没有模型能为放疗预测产生有用的预测结果（因为放疗决策的关键驱动因素是基因表达数据中未捕获的临床变量）。最显著的是，GraphPath在前列腺癌靶向分子治疗预测中达到0.92的AUROC，为整个基准测试中的最高分，表明在仅有11%阳性率的极端类别不平衡条件下，当匹配到具有狭窄可靶向驱动程序队列时，侧向共调控结构能产生卓越的判别能力。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了通路引导治疗反应建模的首个统一基准，证明了不同模型在不同临床任务中的优势互补性。研究揭示了基因表达数据在放疗预测中的局限性，并展示了GraphPath在特定癌症类型（前列腺癌）中的优异表现，为未来多任务临床预测系统的开发提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Cancer treatment planning requires decisions across multiple clinical dimensions at once. Clinicians must determine whether a patient should receive targeted molecular therapy, radiation therapy, and whether they are likely to survive beyond six months. Existing pathway-informed deep learning models have been developed and tested in isolation, making fair comparison across architectures impossible. We present the first unified benchmark for pathway-guided therapy response modeling, evaluating three biologically informed architectures, BINN, GraphPath, and PATH, across five cancer cohorts drawn from The Cancer Genome Atlas, representing 2,622 patients encoded using Reactome pathway activity scores. Each model is trained jointly on all three clinical outcomes under identical data and evaluation conditions, the first study to treat pathway-structured deep learning as a combined therapy and survival prediction problem. Our results show that no single architecture wins across all tasks: PATH performs best for targeted molecular therapy prediction overall, BINN is most reliable for survival prediction, and no model produces useful predictions for radiation therapy, as the key drivers of that decision are clinical variables not captured in gene expression data. Most strikingly, GraphPath achieves an AUROC of 0.92 on prostate targeted molecular therapy prediction, the highest score in the entire benchmark, demonstrating that lateral co-regulation structure produces exceptional discriminative power when matched to a cohort with a narrow targetable driver programme, even under conditions of extreme class imbalance at only 11\% positive prevalence.

</details>


### [79] [Uncertainty-aware Multi-fidelity Closure via Conditional Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09857)
*Jice Zeng,Shady E. Ahmed,David Barajas-Solano,Panos Stinis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件归一化流的多保真度学习框架，用于解决降阶模型（ROM）的闭合问题，通过从低保真度ROM系数学习到高保真度系数的概率映射来提高预测精度并量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降阶模型（ROMs）虽然为复杂多尺度系统提供了高效的替代模型，但其预测精度经常受到截断误差和已解析尺度与未解析尺度之间相互作用表征不足的限制。这种被截断（未解析）尺度对ROM（已解析）尺度的缺失影响通常被称为闭合问题，严重影响了ROM的预测准确性。

Method: 将ROM闭合建模表述为一个多保真度（MF）学习问题，提出了一种基于条件归一化流的不确定性感知MF框架。该方法学习从低保真度（LF）ROM系数到高保真度（HF）系数的概率映射。研究了两类校正策略：（1）直接学习：直接从LF输入预测HF系数；（2）残差学习：学习LF和HF系数之间的差异，并利用其恢复校正后的HF解。

Result: 在由二维Navier-Stokes方程控制的涡旋合并问题上进行验证，结果显示两种校正策略均提高了ROM相对于未校正ROM的准确性，其中残差学习的表现始终优于直接学习。此外，所提出的两种基于深度生成模型的策略能够为校正后的ROM系数提供不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 基于深度生成模型的多保真度学习框架能够有效解决ROM闭合问题，显著提升预测精度，同时提供不确定性量化能力，这对评估预测置信度和支持ROM在实际应用中的可靠使用至关重要。

Abstract: Reduced-order models (ROMs) provide an efficient surrogate for complex multiscale systems, but their predictive accuracy is often compromised by truncation errors and the inadequate representation of interactions between resolved and unresolved scales. The missing effect of truncated (unresolved) scales on ROM (resolved) scales is often denoted as the closure problem. In this work, we formulate ROM closure modeling as a multi-fidelity (MF) learning problem and propose an uncertainty-aware MF framework based on conditional normalizing flow to enhance ROM predictive accuracy. The proposed approach learns a probabilistic mapping from low-fidelity (LF) ROM coefficients to high-fidelity (HF) coefficients, thereby improving predictive fidelity while quantifying the uncertainty associated with the learned closure. Two correction strategies are investigated: direct learning, in which HF coefficients are predicted directly from LF inputs, and residual learning, which learns the discrepancy between LF and HF coefficients and uses it to recover the corrected HF solution. The framework is demonstrated on a vortex merging problem governed by the two-dimensional Navier Stokes equations. Results show that both correction strategies improve ROM accuracy over uncorrected ROM, with residual learning achieving consistently better performance than direct learning. Moreover, the two proposed deep generative model-based strategies provide uncertainty quantification for the corrected ROM coefficients, which is critical for assessing prediction confidence and supporting the reliable use of ROMs in practical applications.

</details>


### [80] [Co-GLANCE: Uncertainty-Aware Active Perception for Heterogeneous Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09919)
*Michal P. Podolinsky,Neel P. Bhatt,Pranay Samineni,Rohan Siva,Christian Ellis,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Co-GLANCE是一种面向异构机器人团队的实时机载感知与决策系统，通过将视觉语言模型的语义推理能力蒸馏到端到端模型中，并结合共形预测与选择性弃权机制，实现了在遮挡分割与机器人分配上的高精度与低延迟，显著优于云端VLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化户外环境中，异构机器人团队面临感知不确定性这一核心挑战，单一视角难以可靠理解场景。现有视觉语言模型虽具备强语义先验，但计算成本高且缺乏校准的不确定性量化，难以支持实时机载推理与可靠决策。

Method: Co-GLANCE将视觉语言模型的语义推理能力蒸馏至端到端模型，实现遮挡分割与机器人分配，无需云端推理；引入共形预测与选择性弃权，为分割、分配与检测输出提供统计有效的覆盖保证；基于校准的不确定性估计触发主动感知，派遣最合适的机器人获取信息丰富的视角以消除不确定性。

Result: 在真实场景中，Co-GLANCE在遮挡分割与机器人分配精度上分别比云端视觉语言模型基线提升25%与36%，每帧推理延迟降低350倍。同时公开了一个空-地数据集以支持后续研究。

Conclusion: Co-GLANCE有效解决了异构机器人团队在非结构化户外环境中的感知不确定性问题，实现了实时、可校准且高性能的机载感知与决策，为未来研究提供了系统与数据支持。

Abstract: Perceptual uncertainty is a central challenge for heterogeneous robot teams operating in unstructured outdoor environments, where no single viewpoint affords reliable scene understanding. Perceptual uncertainty, arising from sources such as occlusions, manifests differently across robot viewpoints depending on scene structure. Detecting and resolving sources of perceptual uncertainty requires both scene-based contextual reasoning and capability-aware robot allocation. While vision-language models provide strong semantic priors for both, they are computationally prohibitive for onboard inference and lack calibrated uncertainty quantification. We introduce Co-GLANCE, a real-time onboard perception and decision-making system for uncertainty resolution in heterogeneous robot teams. Co-GLANCE distills the semantic reasoning capabilities of a vision-language model into an end-to-end model for occlusion segmentation and robot allocation, eliminating the need for cloud-based inference. To quantify perceptual uncertainty, Co-GLANCE combines conformal prediction with selective abstention to provide statistically valid coverage guarantees for segmentation, robot allocation, and detection outputs. These calibrated uncertainty estimates directly trigger active perception, dispatching the most appropriate robot to acquire informative viewpoints and resolve uncertainty. Across real-world scenarios, Co-GLANCE outperforms cloud-based vision-language model baselines in occlusion segmentation and robot allocation accuracy by 25% and 36%, respectively, while reducing per-frame inference latency 350x. We also release an air-ground dataset for future research. Code, videos, and dataset available at https://co-glance.github.io/ .

</details>


### [81] [Mitigating Manifold Departure: Uncertainty-Aware Subspace Rectification for Trustworthy MLLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09859)
*Yingxuan Zhuang,Jingxiao Yang,Miao Pan,Cheng Tan,Yuxiang Cai,Siwei Tan,Chen Zhi,Xuhong Zhang,Jianwei Yin,Jintao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MGAP方法，通过几何感知的自适应投影来缓解MLLMs中的幻觉问题，同时保持语义连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练解码策略通过惩罚语言先验来减少幻觉，但忽略了语言先验的双重性（既可能有害也可能有益），盲目抑制会破坏模型的语义流形，导致性能下降。

Method: MGAP通过SVD从盲隐状态构建语言先验子空间；在解码时将多模态隐状态投影到该子空间，并应用一致性感知门控机制自适应地衰减投影先验分量，从而保留正交的语义分量。

Result: 在POPE和CHAIR基准测试上，MGAP在幻觉抑制方面优于先前的解码基线方法，同时保持了生成内容的连贯性。

Conclusion: MGAP通过几何感知的自适应投影方法有效缓解了MLLMs中的幻觉问题，同时避免了盲目抑制语言先验导致的性能下降，实现了更好的平衡。

Abstract: MLLMs frequently hallucinate objects inconsistent with visual inputs. This issue is typically attributed to the over-reliance on language priors, which can override the visual context. Recent training-free decoding strategies address this by penalizing language priors. However, these methods overlook the dual nature of language priors, where they can be both helpful and harmful depending on the alignment with visual evidence. In particular, blindly suppressing language priors often disrupts the model's semantic manifold, leading to performance degradation, a phenomenon we term Manifold Departure. To address this, we propose Manifold-Guided Adaptive Projection (MGAP), a geometry-aware, training-free decoding method that mitigates hallucinations while preserving representation structure. MGAP first constructs a language-prior subspace from blind hidden states via SVD. During decoding, MGAP projects each multimodal hidden state onto this subspace and applies a consistency-aware gate to adaptively attenuate only the projected prior component, yielding a subspace-selective update that largely preserves the orthogonal semantic components. Extensive experiments on POPE and CHAIR show that MGAP outperforms prior decoding baselines, achieving stronger hallucination suppression without sacrificing coherence.

</details>


### [82] [Conformal Risk Prediction for Non-Alcoholic Fatty Liver Disease Using Gradient Boosting with Distribution-Free Coverages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09860)
*Xinze Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合梯度提升决策树和保形预测的机器学习框架，用于NAFLD风险预测，在多中心队列中达到0.912的AUROC，并提供具有统计保证的校准风险估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非酒精性脂肪性肝病（NAFLD）影响约25%的全球成年人，带来显著的肝脏和心血管风险，但目前缺乏有效的群体水平筛查工具，因此需要开发准确且可解释的风险预测方法。

Method: 提出了一种机器学习框架，结合梯度提升决策树与保形预测（conformal prediction）来生成校准的、无分布假设的风险估计覆盖保证；引入基于互信息的稳定性选择程序，通过Bootstrap重采样识别紧凑且临床可解释的特征子集。

Result: 在广州多中心队列（主要队列n=2,187，外部验证n=412）上评估，使用78个候选特征，内部AUROC达0.912，外部达0.891，优于深度神经网络、TabNet、支持向量机和逻辑回归；保形预测集在90%名义水平下达到91.3%的经验覆盖率；三级风险分层显示高危组的12个月进展率是低危组的4.7倍。

Conclusion: 该方法成功开发了高性能的NAFLD风险预测模型，具有良好的校准性和可解释性，所选特征（腰围、ALT、GGT、甘油三酯、空腹血糖和BMI）与已知的代谢危险因素一致，具有生物学合理性。

Abstract: Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease (NAFLD) affects roughly 25% of global adults, posing substantial hepatic and cardiovascular risks. Yet, population-level screening tools remain inadequate. We present Method, a machine-learning framework for NAFLD risk prediction coupling gradient-boosted decision trees with conformal prediction to yield calibrated, distribution-free coverage guarantees on individual risk estimates. It integrates a mutual-information-based stability selection procedure to identify a compact, clinically interpretable feature subset via bootstrap resampling, constructing prediction sets whose marginal coverage provably exceeds a user-specified confidence level. We evaluated Method on a multicenter cohort from Guangzhou, China (primary n=2,187; external validation n=412) using 78 candidate features across demographics, metabolic biomarkers, and lifestyle factors. Method achieves an AUROC of 0.912 internally and 0.891 externally, outperforming deep neural networks, TabNet, support vector machines, and logistic regression. Conformal prediction sets achieve 91.3% empirical coverage at the 90% nominal level. A three-tier risk stratification derived from these scores separates the population into distinct groups, with the high-risk subgroup showing a 12-month progression rate 4.7 times that of the low-risk tier. The selected features -- notably waist circumference, ALT, GGT, triglycerides, fasting glucose, and BMI -- align with established metabolic risk factors, providing biological plausibility.

</details>


### [83] [Time Series as Language: A Universal Tokenizer for General-Purpose Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09861)
*Yunhao Zhang,Ruiying Qi,Jiale Zheng,Jianfeng Zhang,Lujia Pan,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UniTok tokenizer和UniTok-FM基础模型，通过将连续时间序列离散化并应用Next-Token Prediction预训练，实现了跨任务（预测、生成、分类）的零样本/少样本学习，无需针对TS修改LLM架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Next-Token Prediction虽已统一LLM预训练，但将其适配到无界连续时间序列仍是一个开放问题。现有方法难以支持跨任务的零样本/少样本推理能力。

Method: 1. UniTok：向量量化自编码器，包含前缀归一化（尺度稳定）、渐进分辨率因果编解码架构、结构保持重建损失；2. UniTok-FM：直接使用现成LLM架构，在具有相似模式的多个序列组成的上下文窗口上进行NTP预训练，捕捉共享动态。

Result: 单一统一的UniTok-FM在预测、生成、分类任务上持续优于统计和监督基线，与任务特定基础模型达到竞争性能，并独特地实现了跨任务的无训练上下文推理能力。

Conclusion: UniTok成功弥合了NTP与时间序列之间的差距，证明了无需TS特定修改的通用LLM架构可以通过token化方法在时间序列多任务场景中取得优异且统一的表现。

Abstract: While Next-Token Prediction (NTP) has unified LLM pretraining, its adaptation to unbounded, continuous time series (TS) remains open. To bridge the gap, we introduce UniTok, a universal tokenizer that transforms TS into discrete tokens, and UniTok-FM, a foundation model pretrained via NTP on these tokens. UniTok-FM is a general-purpose foundation model that supports zero-shot and prompt-boosted forecasting, as well as few-shot generation and classification via training-free in-context inference--a capability not achieved by prior works. Technically, UniTok is a vector-quantized autoencoder incorporating prefix normalization for scale stabilization, a progressive-resolution causal architecture for encoding and decoding, and a structure-preserving reconstruction loss for training. UniTok-FM adopts an off-the-shelf LLM architecture without TS-specific modifications. Instead of pretraining on isolated TS, it performs NTP on context windows formed by multiple series with similar patterns, aiming to capture their shared dynamics. Experiments on forecasting, generation, and classification show that a single unified UniTok-FM consistently outperforms statistical and supervised baselines, achieves competitive performance with task-specific foundation models, and uniquely enables training-free in-context inference across tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Blurry Window Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09862)
*Axel Laborieux,Christos Sourmpis,Juan Gabriel Kostelec,Qinghai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了模糊窗口注意力（BLA），一种新的线性复杂度注意力机制，通过Dirichlet核插值重建模糊KV历史，在序列长度上实现了线性的计算复杂度。在MQAR任务中状态效率比滑动窗口注意力高8倍，在RegBench任务中是唯一随状态规模增长性能提升的线性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型中的Softmax Attention操作在序列长度上具有二次复杂度，且KV缓存状态规模不断增长，成为长上下文场景的瓶颈。尽管现有的线性复杂度替代架构（如SSMs、Linear Attention、ABC）能达到与Transformer相似的语言困惑度，但在需要检索或回忆特定信息的任务上仍落后于Transformer。

Method: 提出模糊窗口注意力（BLA），一种受状态空间模型启发的ABC方法。BLA存储频率窗口，通过Dirichlet核插值重建模糊KV历史。该方法可理解为滑动窗口注意力的泛化（取决于Dirichlet核分辨率），或门控槽注意力的特例（其中衰减因子由Dirichlet核实现）。论文详细阐述了BLA理论和高效实现。

Result: 在Multi-Query Associate Recall合成任务中，BLA的状态效率比SWA高8倍，与流行的线性注意力模型具有竞争力；在RegBench合成任务中，在测试的线性模型中，只有BLA和SWA随着状态规模增长而提升性能。

Conclusion: BLA成功结合了线性复杂度的计算优势与有效的信息检索能力，在保持有限状态规模的同时，显著提升了需要特定信息回忆任务的性能，为长上下文语言建模提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: The Softmax Attention operation in Transformer language models has a quadratic complexity in the sequence length and a growing state size in the form of KV cache, which becomes a bottleneck in long context scenarios. To overcome this limitation, alternative architectures with linear complexity and finite state size have been introduced, such as State-Space Models (SSMs), Linear Attention (LA), and Attention with Bounded-memory Control (ABC). Though linear models achieve similar language perplexity as Transformers, they are still behind in tasks which require retrieval or recall of specific information. In this work, we introduce Blurry Window Attention (BLA) a novel ABC method inspired by SSMs. BLA stores a frequency window from which a blurry KV history is reconstructed via interpolation using Dirichlet kernels. BLA can be understood as a generalization of Sliding Window Attention (SWA) depending on the Dirichlet kernels resolution or as a special case of the Gated Slot Attention (GSA), where the decay factor is implemented with Dirichlet kernels. We describe in details the theory and efficient implementation of BLA. On the Multi-Query Associate Recall (MQAR) synthetic task, we show that the state efficiency of BLA is 8$\times$ better than SWA and is competitive with popular linear attention models, and in the RegBench synthetic task, only BLA and SWA improve their performance as the state size grows among the linear models we tested.

</details>


### [85] [From Confident Closing to Silent Failure: Characterizing False Success in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09863)
*Laksh Advani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM代理存在“虚假成功”问题（声称完成任务但实际未完成）。研究发现此现象普遍存在，LLM评估器检测能力差（AUROC最高仅0.65）。轻量级TF-IDF检测器表现优异，在相同标记率下比最佳评估器多恢复4-8倍的虚假成功案例，且延迟降低3300倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理可能发生“无声失败”，即声称任务完成但环境状态显示未完成（虚假成功）。现有研究缺乏对此类失败模式的系统性分析，生产环境需要有效的监控方法来识别这些虚假成功案例。

Method: 在两个基准测试上系统研究虚假成功现象：tau2-bench（9,876条轨迹，8个模型家族）和AppWorld（1,879条轨迹，4个模型家族，含文本无关真值）。评估5种LLM评估器、5种提示策略的检测效果，并开发轻量级TF-IDF检测器进行对比。分析评估器依赖的判断特征。

Result: 虚假成功率因场景差异显著：tau2-bench单控制域45-48%，双控制电信域3%，AppWorld自评估编码代理75.8%。LLM评估器表现不佳：tau2-bench上AUROC不超过0.65，AppWorld仅0.54。评估器依赖表面特征（闭合语言自信度、动作序列量）而非验证状态变化。轻量级TF-IDF检测器在tau2-bench达AUROC 0.83，AppWorld达0.95，延迟降低3300倍，相同标记率下恢复4-8倍更多虚假成功。

Conclusion: 生产监控不应依赖LLM评估器作为虚假成功的主要监控手段，而应使用轻量级、领域校准的检测器作为分诊信号。研究揭示了LLM代理评估的固有缺陷，为构建可靠的生产监控系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents can fail silently by asserting task completion when the environment state shows otherwise. We study this failure mode, false success, across two agent benchmarks: 9,876 tau2-bench trajectories from 8 model families and 1,879 AppWorld trajectories from 4 model families with text-independent ground truth. False success is common but varies by setting: 45--48% of failures in single-control tau2-bench domains, 3% in dual-control telecom, and 75.8% among AppWorld self-assessing coding-agent trajectories with explicit status claims. LLM judges fail reliably: no configuration across 5 judges, 5 prompt strategies, and full task specifications exceeds AUROC 0.65 on tau2-bench, and the same judges reach only 0.54 AUROC on AppWorld API-call traces. Judges rely on surface completion proxies -- confident closing language in tau2-bench and coarse action-sequence volume in AppWorld -- rather than verified state changes. Lightweight TF-IDF detectors achieve task-disjoint AUROC 0.83 on tau2-bench and 0.95 on AppWorld, recovering 4--8x more false successes than the best judge at the same flag rate with 3,300x lower latency. These results suggest that production monitoring should use lightweight, domain-calibrated detectors as triage signals rather than relying on LLM judges as the primary monitor for false success.

</details>


### [86] [Alignment Collapse Under KV Cache Quantization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09864)
*Bruce Changlong Xu,Adarsh Kumarappan,Mu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现低比特KV缓存量化会隐蔽破坏大模型的安全对齐，提出了一种无需训练的诊断方法PCR，能预测失效模式并指导恢复，在多种模型和量化器上实现了高达97%的安全对齐恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存量化评估仅关注困惑度和准确率，忽略了其对模型安全对齐的潜在破坏，缺乏对量化后安全失效的理解和应对机制。

Method: 揭示了安全失效的几何根源（安全特征位于对量化噪声极度敏感的低维激活子空间），提出Per-Channel Reduction（PCR）诊断方法，将模型分类为三种机制失效模式，并据此指导针对性的缓解策略。

Result: 在11个指令微调模型和5个基准测试中发现：Mistral-7B在仅1.03倍困惑度下丧失15.2%的拒绝能力；PCR在所有9个主模型和1个独立族外部模型上正确预测缓解方向，在未见提示、模型和生产量化器上实现高达97.2%的对齐恢复。

Conclusion: KV缓存量化存在模型特异的尖锐相变安全风险，标准指标无法检测；PCR诊断方法能在约35 GPU分钟内以最小内存开销恢复高达97%的丢失对齐，成功解决了生产环境中FP8 KV缓存确认的脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache quantization is widely used to reduce Large Language Model (LLM) inference memory, yet existing evaluations solely focus on measuring perplexity and accuracy without assessing the safety impact. In this study, we explore alignment preservation under KV cache quantization. Across eleven instruction-tuned models (3.8B-72B) and five benchmarks (1,894 prompts), we find that low-bit quantization can silently destroy safety alignment: Mistral-7B loses 15.2% of its refusals at only 1.03x perplexity, and no universal safe bit-width exists, with sharp model-specific phase transitions invisible to standard metrics. We identify that the root cause is geometric: safety features occupy a low-dimensional activation subspace 10^2-10^3x more vulnerable to quantization noise than the full representation space perplexity averages over. Inspired by this observation, we propose Per-Channel Reduction (PCR), a diagnostic that classifies each model into one of three mechanistic failure modes: outlier-crushes-safety, where safety lives in non-outlier channels collaterally damaged by outlier-driven scale factors; outlier-as-safety, where safety overlaps outlier channels and finer granularity cannot rescue it; and multi-layer dilution, where safety is distributed across many layers and per-layer fixes fail. PCR predicts the correct mitigation direction on all nine primary models and one held-out model from an independent family using 20 calibration prompts. PCR generalizes across unseen prompts, models, and production quantizers, including KIVI with up to 97.2% recovery, succeeding where attention-based allocation methods fail. The resulting training-free protocol, requiring approximately 35 GPU-minutes, recovers up to 97% of lost alignment at minimal memory overhead, addressing vulnerabilities confirmed in production vLLM serving with FP8 KV cache on NVIDIA GPUs.

</details>


### [87] [LLM-as-a-Discriminator: When Synthetic Tables Still Look Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09865)
*Manel Slokom,Malek Slokom,Thierno Kante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用大语言模型（LLM）作为判别器来审计表格合成数据的隐私风险。实验表明LLM能有效区分真实数据与合成数据，其性能因模型选择、数据集和评估设置而异，可作为实用的隐私审计信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护与数据共享之间存在张力。虽然合成数据被广泛用于降低隐私风险，但对表格数据的隐私审计仍然困难，甚至人类也难以区分真实表格与合成表格。因此需要一种有效的自动化方法来评估合成数据的隐私风险。

Method: 提出基于LLM判别的方法：让LLM对每个表格样本进行二分类（真实/合成）。测试两种设置：C1（仅表格）和C2（表格+分布元数据）。使用LLaMA作为开源模型、Gemini作为参考模型。在UCI Adult和ACS Census两个公开数据集上测试三种合成模型（CTGAN、TVAE、Gaussian Copula），收集451个有效试验。并与分类器双样本测试（C2ST）、记录链接方法以及人类标注基线进行对比。

Result: 模型间表现差异明显：在Adult数据集上，LLaMA在报告单元格中达到DRS=0%，而Gemini对CTGAN和TVAE达到DRS=100%。在Census数据集上，LLaMA将大多数样本预测为合成数据，而Gemini在C1设置中保持高准确率，但在C2设置中对CTGAN和TVAE准确率下降。LLM判别方法在妥善处理模型选择、提供商报告和数据编码后，可作为实用的隐私审计信号。

Conclusion: LLM判别是一种可行的表格数据隐私审计方法，能够有效区分真实数据与合成数据，为合成数据隐私风险评估提供了新的技术途径。研究代码已开源以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Privacy and data sharing are often in tension. Many organizations use synthetic data to reduce privacy risk and still share useful data. For tabular data, auditing privacy remains hard. In many cases, even humans cannot easily tell if a table is real or synthetic. In this paper, we propose a method based on LLM discrimination. We ask an LLM to classify each table sample as REAL or SYNTHETIC. We test two settings: C1 with table only, and C2 with table plus distributional metadata. We use LLaMA as an open model and Gemini as a reference model. In our experiments, we run three synthesis models, CTGAN, TVAE, and Gaussian Copula, on two public datasets, UCI Adult and ACS Census. We collect 451 valid trials. Our results show clear differences between models. On Adult, LLaMA reaches DRS=0% in reported cells, while Gemini reaches DRS=100% for CTGAN and TVAE. On Census, LLaMA predicts SYNTHETIC for most samples, while Gemini stays high in C1 but drops for CTGAN and TVAE in C2. We also compare with a classifier two-sample test (C2ST) and record linkage as distributional baselines, and with a human pilot of 2 annotators and 240 trials. Our results show that LLM discrimination is a practical privacy audit signal when model choice, per provider reporting, and data encoding are handled with care. For reproducibility, code and experiment scripts are available at https://github.com/SlokomManel/LLM-as-a-Discriminator.

</details>


### [88] [Two to Tango: Coupled Task-Reference Selection for Safe LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09866)
*Xinrui Chen,Jianhao Zhang,Ou Wu,Di Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualSelect框架，通过联合选择安全参考样本和兼容任务样本来解决LLM微调中的安全行为退化问题，在保持任务性能的同时显著提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用固定安全样本、全局约束或单向任务过滤，但诊断显示任务更新会暴露不同的安全约束，因此需要联合选择相关参考样本和兼容任务样本来保护已学习的安全行为。

Method: 提出DualSelect耦合框架：在极小极大视角下，通过熵正则化评分代理、惰性参考刷新和梯度校正，选择具有高保留损失和任务冲突的安全参考样本，同时筛选与诱导参考方向兼容的任务样本。

Result: 在1B-8B规模LLM上，DualSelect在保持任务效用的同时保护安全性；使用REDORCA评判器时，安全平均值超过最强基线至少5.10分，在各评判器中保持最高安全平均值，且开销适中。

Conclusion: DualSelect有效解决了LLM微调中的安全性退化问题，该方法还可扩展到以保留为重点的持续学习场景。

Abstract: Fine-tuning safety aligned large language models (LLMs) on downstream data improves adaptation but may erode learned safety behavior. Existing methods use fixed safety examples, global constraints, or one-sided task filtering. Our diagnostics show task updates expose different safety constraints, motivating joint selection of relevant references and compatible task samples. We propose DualSelect, a coupled framework for task and reference selection that refreshes task conditioned safety references before filtering whole task samples compatible with the induced reference direction. Under a minimax view, DualSelect selects safety references with high preservation loss and task conflict, together with compatible task samples, through entropy-regularized scoring surrogates, lazy reference refresh, and gradient correction. On 1B-8B LLMs, DualSelect preserves safety without losing task utility; using the REDORCA judge, it improves Safety Avg. over the strongest baseline by at least 5.10 points and remains highest in Safety Avg. across judges with moderate overhead. This view extends to retention focused continual learning.

</details>


### [89] [Inverse Probability Weighting and Age-of-Information Aggregation for Decentralized Federated Learning under Partial Reception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10774)
*Chanuka A. S. Hewa Kaluannakkage,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DFL-AA框架，通过逆概率加权和AoI加权机制，有效解决了有损无线网络中分布式联邦学习面临的选择偏差和更新过时问题，无需全局同步即可实现性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有损无线网络中的分布式联邦学习（DFL）面临两个关键挑战：选择偏差（由于部分模型接收导致低质量链路的更新被系统性低估）和更新过时（异步节点提供过时信息）。现有方法如均匀聚合和基于完整性的加权会引入或放大链路质量偏差。

Method: 提出DFL-AA方法，结合逆概率加权（Inverse Probability Weighting）与基于EWMA的在线信道估计来纠正选择偏差，并采用信息年龄加权来缓解更新过时问题，无需全局同步。

Result: 理论上证明DFL-AA能够期望消除链路质量失真；实验上在变化的丢包率、网络规模和异构无线条件下，相比最先进的基线方法表现出一致的性能改进。

Conclusion: DFL-AA成功解决了有损无线网络中DFL的选择偏差和更新过时两大核心挑战，为实际部署提供了理论保证和实验验证。

Abstract: Decentralized Federated Learning (DFL) over lossy wireless networks faces two key challenges: selection bias, where updates from poor-quality links are systematically underrepresented due to partial model reception, and update staleness, where asynchronous nodes contribute outdated information. We show that uniform gossip aggregation with local-fill reconstruction introduces persistent link-quality-induced bias, while completeness-based weighting further amplifies this effect. To address these challenges, we propose DFL-AA (Decentralized Federated Learning with Adaptive AoI-weighted Aggregation), which combines Inverse Probability Weighting with online EWMA-based channel estimation to correct selection bias and Age-of-Information-based weighting to mitigate staleness without requiring global synchronization. We theoretically show that DFL-AA removes link-quality distortion in expectation and experimentally demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across varying loss rates, network sizes, and heterogeneous wireless conditions.

</details>


### [90] [SPACE: Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09868)
*Zhijing Zhang,Jiaqi Ding,Qianshan Wei,Nan Zhou,Jiaqi Li,Yongliang Wu,Tongxin Zhu,Xiaolin Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPACE，首个面向多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的无源数据遗忘框架，在无需目标概念视觉数据的情况下，通过文本引导的代理锚点选择与双约束语义隔离，实现了与依赖数据的最先进方法相当的概念擦除性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大语言模型面临日益增长的隐私风险与监管约束，机器遗忘（MU）成为移除敏感数据同时保持模型性能的关键解决方案。然而，现有MU方法通常依赖目标概念的视觉数据，而严格的数据保留政策往往导致这些数据不可用，因此亟需无需访问目标数据的无源遗忘方法。

Method: 提出Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure (SPACE)框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 文本引导代理锚点选择（TPAS），从共享特征空间中检索语义对齐的代理锚点；(2) 双约束语义隔离（DCSI），优化这些锚点以间接擦除目标概念。DCSI将更新限制在保留知识的零空间中，确保结构完整性。理论上证明了SPACE严格界定对保留知识的扰动并最大化特征谱熵。

Result: 在六个数据集上的广泛实验表明，SPACE实现了与依赖数据的最先进方法相当的性能，验证了其在无源MU场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPACE作为首个面向MLLM的无源遗忘框架，成功解决了目标视觉数据不可用场景下的概念擦除问题，在保持模型性能的同时有效移除敏感信息，为隐私保护与合规部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face growing privacy risks and regulatory constraints, machine unlearning (MU) has emerged as a crucial solution for removing sensitive data while preserving model performance. However, existing MU methods typically rely on visual data of the target concepts, which is often unavailable due to strict data retention policies, thus creating a demand for source-free unlearning approaches that operate without access to the target data. In this work, we propose Source-free Proxy Anchor Concept Erasure (SPACE), the first source-free unlearning framework specialized for MLLMs. SPACE consists of two stages: (1) Text-Guided Proxy Anchor Selection (TPAS), which retrieves semantically aligned proxy anchors from the shared feature space. (2) Dual-Constraint Semantic Isolation (DCSI), which optimizes these anchors to indirectly erase target concepts. DCSI confines updates to the null space of retained knowledge, ensuring structural integrity. We theoretically prove that SPACE strictly bounds the perturbation on retained knowledge and maximizes feature spectral entropy, thereby maintaining the model's performance. Furthermore, extensive experiments across six datasets show that SPACE achieves performance comparable to that of state-of-the-art data-dependent methods, validating its effectiveness in source-free MU scenarios. The source code will be released.

</details>


### [91] [QSplitFL: Capability Aware Deep Q-Learning for Optimal Split Point Selection in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09869)
*Nazmus Shakib Shadin,Xinyue Zhang,Jingyi Wang,Miao Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QSplitFL框架，利用基于硬件指标的轻量级状态表示和委员会DQN架构，在联邦分割学习环境中实现最优分割点选择，适应异构设备资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习与分割学习的结合虽能降低训练成本并保护隐私，但最优分割点的确定仍是一个关键挑战。固定分割点会导致弱设备过载、增加通信和服务器负载，从而减慢收敛速度并降低稳定性，特别是在客户端具有异构硬件能力的情况下。

Method: 提出QSplitFL框架，采用基于客户端硬件指标（CPU利用率、内存、电量、网络延迟）的轻量级状态表示，而非高维模型权重表示。引入衰减损失下降奖励函数以优先考虑早期收敛，并采用带多数投票的委员会DQN架构来缓解奖励欺骗问题。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，使用CNN、ResNet50、MobileNetV4和ConvNeXt架构进行大量实验。结果表明，该方法相比现有方法实现了更好的收敛性和更高的准确率，同时能有效适应异构设备资源。

Conclusion: QSplitFL通过硬件感知的轻量级状态表示和委员会DQN架构，成功解决了联邦分割学习中的最优分割点选择问题，在异构设备环境下实现了更优的收敛性能和准确率。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) combined with Split Learning (SL) is a privacy preserving paradigm that enables training deep neural networks (DNNs) on resource constrained devices while reducing overall training cost. However, determining the optimal split point, meaning the layer where the model is divided still remains a critical challenge, especially when clients have heterogeneous hardware capabilities. Fixed split points can overload weak devices and increase the communication and server load, which slows convergence and reduces stability. This paper introduces QSplitFL, a novel capability-aware Deep Q-Network (DQN) framework for optimal split point selection in Split learning based Federated Learning (SFL) environments. Unlike existing approaches that rely on high-dimensional model weight representations, QSplitFL employs a lightweight state representation derived directly from client hardware metrics, including CPU utilization, memory, battery level, and network latency. The proposed framework incorporates a decayed loss-drop reward function that prioritizes early convergence, and a committee-based DQN architecture with majority voting to mitigate reward hacking. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 datasets using CNN, ResNet50, MobileNetV4, and ConvNeXt architectures demonstrate that our approach achieves better convergence and higher accuracy compared to existing methods, while effectively adapting to heterogeneous device resources. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/AIPO-Lab/QSplitFL.

</details>


### [92] [LLM-Guided Neural Architecture Search for Robust Co-Design of Physical Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10294)
*Tyler King,Timothee Leleu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了UH-NAS框架，一个硬件无关、LLM引导的神经架构搜索方法，通过可替换的硬件后端实现跨平台公平比较，在光学MZI硬件上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经架构搜索（NAS）方法通常针对单一硬件系列定制，限制了跨平台比较和泛化能力。在非传统硬件上部署神经网络需要协同优化任务精度和特定平台约束（如能耗、物理非理想性、数值精度），但缺乏统一的跨平台搜索框架。

Method: 提出非传统硬件神经架构搜索（UH-NAS）框架：1）硬件无关设计，将硬件作为可替换后端；2）集成大语言模型作为进化算子进行架构搜索；3）每个硬件后端包含平台特定的能耗模型、物理约束和非理想性模拟器；4）协同优化精度和推理能耗。

Result: 在光学MZI硬件上测试，UH-NAS相比传统基线发现了更多样化、更鲁棒的架构，并且优于现有的LLM-to-NAS方法。架构在非理想条件下的鲁棒性消融实验和系统提示的作用分析验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 架构-硬件协同设计对新兴计算平台至关重要。UH-NAS通过硬件后端抽象实现了跨平台公平比较，无需修改搜索算法即可适配不同硬件平台，为非传统硬件上的神经网络部署提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying neural networks on unconventional hardware demands architectures that co-optimize task accuracy and platform-specific constraints such as energy cost, physical non-idealities, and numerical precision. Existing neural architecture search (NAS) methods are typically tailored to a single hardware family, limiting cross-platform comparison and generalization. We introduce Unconventional Hardware Neural Architecture Search (UH-NAS), a hardware-agnostic, LLM-guided NAS framework that integrates language models as evolutionary operators to co-optimize accuracy and inference energy. By exposing hardware as a swappable backend with per-platform energy models, physical constraints, and non-ideality simulators, UH-NAS enables fair system-level comparisons across various backends without modifying the search algorithm. Tested on optical MZI hardware, UH-NAS discovers more diverse, robust architectures than conventional baselines while outperforming existing LLM-to-NAS approaches. Additional ablations on architecture robustness under non-idealities and the role of system prompts highlight the importance of architecture-hardware co-design for emerging computing platforms.

</details>


### [93] [PatchSTG: Scalable Spatiotemporal Graph Transformers for Traffic Forecasting on Irregular Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09872)
*Jichao Li,Xuanming Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PatchSTG模型，通过将不均匀分布的传感器划分为局部保持的patch，使用双注意力机制实现从二次到近线性的计算复杂度，在保持预测精度的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通网络中传感器分布不均匀，导致空间结构非均匀，限制了现有基于图和注意力模型的有效性和可扩展性，且大规模时空依赖建模的计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出PatchSTG模型：基于地理信息将传感器划分为平衡且保持局部性的patch，构建层次化空间表示；设计双注意力编码器，交替使用patch内注意力捕获局部交互和patch间注意力建模全局依赖，将计算复杂度从二次降低到近线性。

Result: 在罗德岛真实交通数据和大规模数据集上的实验表明，模型在多个预测时段保持稳定且有竞争力的预测性能，同时显著提升计算效率；消融实验验证了空间分区和双注意力机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于patch的时空建模为不规则空间设置下的交通预测提供了一个可扩展且有效的框架。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is a fundamental component of intelligent transportation systems, yet remains challenging in real-world settings due to irregular sensor distributions and the high computational cost of modeling large-scale spatiotemporal dependencies. In practical traffic networks, sensors are unevenly distributed across regions, leading to non-uniform spatial structures that limit the effectiveness and scalability of existing graph-based and attention-based models. To address these challenges, we propose PatchSTG, a patch-based spatiotemporal graph Transformer designed for efficient forecasting on irregular sensor networks. The key idea is to introduce a hierarchical spatial representation that partitions sensors into balanced, locality-preserving patches based on geographic information. On top of this structure, a dual attention encoder alternates between intra-patch attention for capturing local interactions and inter-patch attention for modeling global dependencies, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to near-linear scaling. We evaluate PatchSTG on real-world traffic data from Rhode Island and additional large-scale datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model achieves stable and competitive forecasting performance across multiple horizons, while significantly improving computational efficiency. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of spatial partitioning and dual attention in capturing both local and long-range traffic dynamics. These results suggest that patch-based spatiotemporal modeling provides a scalable and effective framework for traffic forecasting under irregular spatial settings.

</details>


### [94] [Rotate2Think: Geometric Priming via Orthogonal Rotation to Improve Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09873)
*Aditya Sharma,Christopher J. Pal,Amal Zouaq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推理模型的输入嵌入和思考嵌入在表示空间中呈高锥度分布且方向不共线，基于此提出无训练方法Rotate2Think，通过正交Procrustes分析估计旋转并在推理时注入合成思考向量，在30/32个配置上提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在生成显式中间推理轨迹时表现优异，但其表示空间的内部结构仍不清晰——模型的隐藏表示在思考过程中与输入提示的嵌入有何差异，以及能否利用这种结构在推理时激发更强的推理能力。

Method: 提出Rotate2Think无训练方法：基于输入嵌入与思考嵌入的高锥度且非共线特性，将输入到思考的转换建模为旋转问题，通过正交Procrustes分析从小量正确样本中估计该旋转，并在推理时在思考分隔符之间注入合成思考向量作为推理轨迹起点的几何引导。

Result: 在多个基准测试和模型家族上的评估显示，Rotate2Think在32个模型-基准配置中的30个上提升了准确率，涵盖数学、科学和代码任务，并零样本泛化至多模态推理基准MATH-Vision。

Conclusion: 输入嵌入与思考嵌入在表示空间中占据几何上截然不同的区域，这一结构特性可通过旋转建模加以利用；Rotate2Think作为一种训练无关的方法，能有效提升推理模型的跨领域推理性能。

Abstract: Reasoning models achieve strong performance on challenging tasks by generating explicit intermediate reasoning traces before producing a final answer. Yet the internal structure of representation space when reasoning remains poorly understood: how do a model's hidden representations differ during thinking versus the embeddings of the input prompt, and can this structure be exploited to elicit stronger reasoning at inference time? We show that both input embeddings and thinking embeddings (mean-pooled last-layer hidden states over the prompt and reasoning trace, respectively) exhibit extremely high conicity, with all vectors clustering tightly around a single mean direction. Crucially, these mean input and thinking directions are non-collinear, with thinking embeddings occupying a geometrically distinct region of embedding space across many different models and benchmark tasks. This observation motivates casting the input-to-thinking transition as a rotation problem admitting a closed-form solution via orthogonal Procrustes analysis. We propose Rotate2Think, a training-free method that estimates this rotation from a small set of correctly solved examples and injects the resulting synthetic thinking vector between thinking delimiters at inference time, providing a geometric primer at the onset of the reasoning trace. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks and model families, Rotate2Think improves accuracy in 30 of 32 model-benchmark configurations across mathematics, science, and code tasks, and generalizes zero-shot to multimodal reasoning on MATH-Vision.

</details>


### [95] [Disjoint or Overlapping? Inference Windowing for Reconstruction-Based Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09874)
*Guillaume Coulaud,Reza Akbarinia,Florent Masseglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于重构的时间序列异常检测方法，发现重叠窗口推理相比非重叠窗口能显著提升性能（平均相对增益达28%），并提出了一个统一的训练、调优和多随机种子评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于重构的时间序列异常检测方法存在评估实践异构和推理过程未明确指定的问题，导致结果难以比较。特别是推理步长（控制子序列是作为不交叠窗口还是重叠窗口处理）的作用尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在精选的TSB-AD基准上提出了统一的训练、调优和多随机种子评估协议，系统研究了重叠推理对多种重构模型的影响，包括基于PCA的基线、DLinear、AutoEncoder、TimesNet和Transformer变体。同时使用与滑动窗口重构对齐的定位标准在完整UCR档案上进行评估。

Result: 在所有模型中，重叠窗口都能带来一致的改进，平均相对增益最高达+28%，并可能改变方法排名。基于重构的基线方法在TSB-AD和UCR基准上都取得了强劲性能。研究还分析了跨数据集、随机种子和超参数配置的变异性。

Conclusion: 基于重构的异常检测性能不仅依赖于模型架构和训练，还依赖于推理选择，这促使需要清晰和可复现的协议。基于重构的基线方法是单变量时间序列异常检测的竞争性和实用方法。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based methods are widely used for time series anomaly detection, where models are trained to reconstruct subsequences, and anomalies are identified through reconstruction errors. However, reported results are often hard to compare due to heterogeneous evaluation practices and underspecified inference procedures. In this paper, we revisit reconstruction-based anomaly detection in the univariate offline setting and study the role of the inference stride, which controls whether subsequences are processed as disjoint windows or with overlap. We propose a unified training, tuning, and multi-seed evaluation protocol on the curated TSB-AD benchmark, and study how overlapping inference affects anomaly detection performance for a range of reconstruction models, including PCA-based baselines, DLinear, an AutoEncoder, TimesNet, and Transformer variants. The results show that across all models, overlapping windows yield consistent improvements, with average relative gain up to +28%, and can alter method rankings. We further analyze variability across datasets, random seeds, and hyperparameter configurations. Finally, we complement the benchmark study with an evaluation on the full UCR archive using localization criteria aligned with sliding-window reconstruction. Overall, our results highlight that reconstruction-based anomaly detection performance depends not only on model architecture and training, but also on inference choices, motivating a clear and reproducible protocol. Our results show that reconstructionbased baselines achieve strong performance on both TSB-AD and UCR benchmarks, supporting them as competitive and practical approaches for univariate time series anomaly detection.

</details>


### [96] [Integrating Local and Global Entropy for Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09875)
*Johanne Medina,Tianyi Zhou,Keivin Isufaj,Aristides Gionis,Sanjay Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GLU方法，通过融合隐藏状态的几何熵（全局不确定性）和token级熵（局部不确定性），有效检测大语言模型中局部信号难以捕捉的“自信但错误”的失败模式，在单次前向传播下实现优于或匹敌无监督基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会产生自信的幻觉，因此不确定性量化对于可靠部署至关重要。现有方法主要依赖token级信号，而对中间隐藏状态的几何结构利用不足。

Method: 将隐藏状态矩阵的几何复杂度作为LLM全局不确定性的度量，token级不确定性估计作为局部度量。提出全局-局部不确定性（GLU）方法，这是一种无监督、单次前向传播的评分方法，通过乘法门控融合全局几何熵和token级熵两种信号。

Result: 隐藏状态几何熵（全局不确定性）和token级熵（局部不确定性）在统计上近乎正交，捕捉不同的失败模式。全局几何能够恢复局部信号系统性遗漏的“自信但错误”的失败模式。在三个模型家族和六个基准测试中，GLU匹配或优于所有无监督基线。

Conclusion: GLU方法仅需要单次前向传播，保持长度归一化和架构无关性，为LLM的可靠性预测提供了一种有效的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate confidently, making uncertainty quantification (UQ) essential for reliable deployment. Existing methods rely predominantly on token-level signals, leaving the geometric structure of intermediate hidden states underused. In this paper, we take the geometric complexity of hidden-state matrices as a measure of the global uncertainty of LLMs, while treating token-level uncertainty estimation as a local metric. We show that hidden-state geometric entropy (global uncertainty) and token-level entropy (local uncertainty) are statistically near-orthogonal, capturing distinct failure regimes for reliability prediction. In particular, global geometry recovers the confident-but-wrong failure mode that local signals systematically miss. Building on this, we propose Global-Local Uncertainty (GLU), an unsupervised, single-pass score that fuses the two signals via a multiplicative gate. Across three model families and six benchmarks, GLU matches or outperforms all unsupervised baselines while requiring only a single forward pass and remaining length-normalized and architecture-agnostic.

</details>


### [97] [Calibrating Overconfidence Without Sacrificing Confidence: Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09876)
*Ke Li,Chongzhe Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Feng Liu,Qunli Zhang,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PCHI方法，通过检测并干预可能错误但高置信度的回答，在不显著损害正确回答置信度的前提下，有效降低了大语言模型的语言化过度自信，将ECE从21.9%降至9.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常对错误答案表现出高置信度，而现有的全局或分数级别的校准方法虽然能降低不当置信度，但也可能侵蚀正确答案的合理置信度，因此需要一种能够选择性降低不当置信度的方法。

Method: 提出Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI)，一种推理时方法：使用冻结探针(frozen probe)检测可能的错误但高置信度响应，并在置信度生成过程中条件性地重新缩放下游注意力头输出，从而实现选择性干预。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上解决OpenMathInstruct问题时，readout-token PCHI将82.2%原本错误的"yes"置信度读取转换为"no"；联合干预将ECE从21.9%降低到9.2%，且仅损害5.1%原本正确的"yes"读取。Gemma3-4B上也观察到类似效果。

Conclusion: 语言化的过度自信可以通过条件性应用的内部干预进行选择性降低，部分实现了将不当置信度的抑制与合理置信度的损失解耦。

Abstract: Large language models often express high confidence in answers that are wrong. Standard calibration remedies typically act globally or at the score level, reducing unwarranted confidence but also risking erosion of warranted confidence on correct answers. We introduce Probe-Conditioned Head Intervention (PCHI), an inference-time method that uses a frozen probe to detect likely wrong-but-confident responses and conditionally rescales downstream attention-head outputs during confidence generation. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct solving OpenMathInstruct problems with a structured binary confidence field, readout-token PCHI converts 82.2% of originally wrong-yes confidence readouts to $\texttt{no}$, while a joint intervention across upstream confidence-template tokens reduces ECE from 21.9% to 9.2% and damages only 5.1% of originally correct-yes readouts. The readout-token effect also appears on Gemma3-4B, though upstream interventions are weaker and more mask-dependent. These results show that verbalized overconfidence can be selectively reduced through conditionally applied internal intervention, partially decoupling the suppression of unwarranted confidence from the loss of warranted confidence.

</details>


### [98] [Streaming Knowledge Compilation: Proactive Materiality-Scored Pinning for Time-Evolving LLM Wikis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09877)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了流式知识编译问题，通过重要性信号$φ_t$动态维护KV缓存，实现了$O(\sqrt{T\log K})$的遗憾界，在金融和维基百科领域验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM wiki系统将知识编译为预填充KV缓存以实现高效推理，但假设语料库是静态的。当底层信息演化时，这一假设就会失效。本文旨在解决动态文档流场景下的知识编译问题：在固定token预算和未知未来查询的条件下，维护一个最小化累积遗憾的编译wiki。

Method: 提出重要性信号$φ_t(k,n)\in[0,1]$来评分实体$k$在时间$t$的文档重要性，作为查询到来前主动固定的查询相关性代理。证明了$O(\sqrt{T\log K})$的遗憾界，其中$\varepsilon=\mathbb{E}[|φ_t-\hatφ_t|]$是唯一的领域特定量。在两个领域实现：金融领域使用冻结的Llama 3.1 8B分类头预测异常股票波动作为$φ_t$（AUROC=0.728）；维基百科领域使用异常编辑率(AER)作为$φ_t$。

Result: 金融领域：在76K文章上AUROC达0.728，预测重要文章的实现前向波动率高1.49倍；累积遗憾收敛至-20.0（-0.12/step）。维基百科领域：累积遗憾收敛至+16.0（+0.13/step），正号确认编辑内容确实在训练后（无wiki得分3.80 vs oracle 4.74）。发现LLM-as-judge在训练后知识上存在普遍混淆，确立了遗憾分析而非绝对QA分数是编译知识系统的可靠评估指标。

Conclusion: $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$的保证适用于任何可以从流信号预测知识缺口的领域。研究建立了遗憾分析作为编译知识系统的可靠评估框架，解决了LLM-as-judge在训练后知识上的混淆问题，证明了统一算法在跨领域场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM wiki systems compile knowledge into pre-filled KV caches for efficient inference, but assume a static corpus -- an assumption that fails whenever the underlying information landscape evolves. We formalize Streaming Knowledge Compilation: given a document stream, a fixed token budget, and future queries unknown at ingestion time, maintain a compiled wiki that minimizes cumulative regret against an offline oracle with perfect foresight. The enabling insight is a materiality signal $φ_t(k,n)\in[0,1]$ that scores document importance for entity $k$ at time $t$, acting as a query-relevance surrogate for proactive pinning before queries arrive; we prove an $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ regret bound where $\varepsilon=\mathbb{E}[|φ_t-\hatφ_t|]$ is the only domain-specific quantity. We instantiate in two domains: finance, where $φ_t$ is abnormal stock volatility predicted by frozen Llama 3.1 8B classification head (AUROC = 0.728 on 76K articles, strict temporal split; $1.49\times$ higher realized forward volatility for predicted-material articles); and Wikipedia, where $φ_t$ is the Abnormal Edit Ratio (AER), a cross-sectionally normalized edit velocity -- showing the same algorithm generalizes beyond the finance domain. End-to-end QA evaluation on 173 matched pairs (finance) and 119 (Wikipedia) reveals a pervasive LLM-as-judge confound on post-training knowledge, establishing that regret analysis -- not absolute QA scores -- is the reliable evaluation metric for compiled knowledge systems. Finance cumulative regret converges to -20.0 (-0.12/step); Wikipedia to +16.0 (+0.13/step), with the positive sign confirming that Wikipedia edit content is genuinely post-training -- richer context consistently improves scores (No Wiki 3.80 vs. Oracle 4.74) -- and eliminates this confound. The $O(\sqrt{T\log K})$ guarantee applies to any domain where knowledge gaps can be predicted from streaming signals.

</details>


### [99] [FailureScope: Cross-Regime Behavioral Diagnosis of Language Model Weaknesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09878)
*Nicholas Saban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FailureScope，一种行为诊断方法，通过跨模型通过/失败模式聚类评估探测任务，为单轮基准、多轮对话和对抗性攻击三种场景生成稳定、可解释的失败分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准测试仅报告聚合准确率，无法帮助实践者了解模型具体缺乏哪些能力，因此需要一种能诊断特定能力缺陷的方法。

Method: 提出FailureScope行为诊断方法，使用留一模型交叉验证（LOMO）技术，根据跨模型的通过/失败模式对评估探测任务进行聚类，生成可解释的失败分类法。

Result: 在2,664个单轮任务上，分类法条件采样在50个任务时达到Kendall's tau=0.81（随机选择仅0.34），跨模型失败预测AUC达0.88；在363任务多轮语料和630条对抗性智能体轨迹上发现元失败模式（LLM-judge ASR与真实执行之间存在73-100个百分点差距）；聚类内聚性在三种场景下均保持强稳定性。

Conclusion: 行为聚类是一种可迁移的诊断原语，能够超越单一基准测试进行泛化，为模型能力缺陷诊断提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Standard benchmarks report aggregate accuracy, but practitioners need to know which specific capabilities a model lacks. We introduce FailureScope, a behavioral-diagnosis method that clusters evaluation probes by their cross-model pass/fail patterns (leave-one-model-out, LOMO), and show it yields stable, interpretable failure taxonomies across three regimes usually studied separately: single-turn benchmarks, multi-turn dialogue, and adversarial agent attacks. On 2,664 single-turn tasks across 18 models, taxonomy-conditioned sampling reaches Kendall's tau = 0.81 at 50 tasks (versus 0.34 for random selection), and cross-model failure prediction reaches AUC 0.88. The same primitive recovers interpretable clusters on a 363-task multi-turn corpus and on 630 adversarial agent traces, where it exposes a meta-failure mode: a 73-100 percentage-point gap between LLM-judge ASR and real execution. Cluster cohesion remains strong across all three regimes, which we take as evidence that behavioral clustering is a portable diagnosis primitive that generalizes beyond any single benchmark. We release the pipeline, three annotated corpora, and the cross-regime taxonomies.

</details>


### [100] [Operator Fusion for LLM Inference on the Tensix Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09879)
*Qingbo Wu,Ke Li,Wenzhu Wang,Jie Yu,Ruian Zhang,Lili Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Tenstorrent Tensix架构上的Transformer模型推理瓶颈，提出算子融合策略，通过将RMSNorm与矩阵乘法融合并利用NoC多播机制，实现了显著的延迟降低，同时保持数值一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在设备端推理时面临性能瓶颈，特别是在Tenstorrent的Tensix架构上，存在数据局部性不足、DRAM带宽竞争严重以及调度开销过大等问题。

Method: 提出算子融合策略，将RMSNorm与自注意力机制和前馈神经网络(FFN)中的矩阵乘法进行融合，在片上SRAM中实现内存受限和计算受限算子的背靠背执行；采用基于NoC的多播机制，通过行/列主节点在核心网格间高效分配输入和权重。

Result: 在Wormhole平台上使用Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-4B模型进行实验，注意力模块延迟降低高达37.44%，MLP模块降低15.89%，每个解码器层降低最高7.91%；皮尔逊相关系数(PCC)保持在98.75%以上。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持数值一致性的前提下，显著提升了端到端推理效率，有效减少了DRAM读写操作和调度开销，为Transformer模型在Tensix架构上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This study addresses on-device inference bottlenecks of Transformer models on Tenstorrent's Tensix architecture and proposes an operator fusion strategy that enhances data locality. RMSNorm is fused with matrix multiplication in self-attention and in the FFN, enabling back-to-back execution of memory-bound and compute-bound operators in on-chip SRAM to significantly reduce DRAM reads/writes of intermediate results and scheduling overhead. To support multi-core parallelism, a NoC-based multicast mechanism is leveraged in which row/column master nodes efficiently distribute inputs and weights across the core mesh, alleviating DRAM bandwidth contention. Experiments on the Wormhole platform with Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen3-0.6B, and Qwen3-4B show up to 37.44% latency reduction for attention and 15.89% for MLP, with up to 7.91% reduction per decoder layer, while Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) remains above 98.75%, confirming significant end-to-end efficiency gains under numerical consistency.

</details>


### [101] [Hyperparameter Learning for Latent Factorization of Tensors for Representation Learning to Large-scale Dynamic Weighted Directed Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09880)
*Yaqian Zhan,Jialan He,Tianzhu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG
