In each period, a customer arrives seeking service, and the platform chooses an assortment of sellers to display. The customer then proposes a transaction to at most one seller in the assortment according to a multinomial logit choice model. After a fixed number of periods, sellers review the proposals they have received and each chooses at most one customer according to another multinomial logit choice model, after which the cycle repeats. A key challenge is that the platform does not know the choice-model parameters of either customers or sellers in advance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of a dynamic assortment problem in which both sides' choice parameters are unknown. We develop a data-driven algorithm that learns these parameters while optimizing the platform's objective over time. We evaluate performance using regret, which measures revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark that knows all parameters and customer arrivals in advance. We show that the algorithm's worst-case regret grows polylogarithmically over time, and we derive a matching lower bound, establishing its rate optimality.

</details>


### [246] [Overcoming Rank Collapse in Feedback Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11123)
*Gauthier Boeshertz,Razvan Pascanu,Claudia Clopath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现反馈对齐(FA)因低秩误差信号难以扩展到深层网络，通过Muon优化器和隐藏层激活正交化提升梯度维度，在ResNet-18上CIFAR100准确率提升9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播因需要反馈权重为前馈权重的转置而被认为生物不可信，FA通过固定随机反馈权重解决此问题，但难以扩展到深层架构。作者旨在探究FA与BP的差异并提出改进方案。

Method: 1) 分析BP与FA模型在CIFAR10上的有效秩差异，发现FA误差信号秩显著较低；2) 提出两种提升FA有效维度的方法：Muon优化器（正交化权重更新）和隐藏层激活正交化；3) 在多种架构和基准测试上验证。

Result: FA误差信号的低秩特性限制参数空间探索；Muon和激活正交化方法在更大架构上持续超越FA基线，如ResNet-18在CIFAR100上准确率提升9个百分点。

Conclusion: 低维梯度动态是限制FA扩展的关键障碍，诱导高维更新几何结构是扩展反向传播替代方案的有前途途径。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) is widely viewed as biologically implausible, in part because it requires feedback weights to be the transpose of forward weights for error propagation. Interestingly, when training a network with fixed random feedback weights to circumvent this issue, learning aligns the forward weights with the feedback weights, leading the backpropagated error signal to become an approximation of the standard gradient used by BP. This process, called Feedback Alignment (FA), occurs in MLPs and very shallow CNNs but does not scale well to deeper architectures. In this work, we first investigated differences between BP and FA models, trained on CIFAR10, specifically focusing on the effective rank of the signal. We found that the FA error has a considerably lower rank and hence is constrained to a lower-dimensional subspace compared to BP, limiting exploration of the parameter space. Motivated by this observation, we evaluated two mechanisms for increasing the effective dimensionality of FA: Muon, an optimiser that orthogonalises weight updates; and hidden activity normalisation, which promotes activation orthogonality. Across larger architectures and benchmarks, we find that these methods consistently improve over FA baselines, for example, on CIFAR100 with a Resnet-18, accuracy increases by 9 percentage points. Our results identify low-dimensional gradient dynamics as a key obstacle to scaling FA and suggest that inducing higher-dimensional update geometry is a promising route toward scaling alternatives to backpropagation.

</details>


### [247] [Robust Regression of General ReLUs with Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11130)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在交互式学习框架下高斯分布上的ReLU不可知学习问题。提出了一个计算高效的算法，其查询复杂度为$d \cdot polylog(1/ε) + \tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$，并证明了该算法在质量上接近最优，且查询访问对于改善被动学习的标签复杂度是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动学习在处理高斯分布下的ReLU学习时需要大量的标记样本。交互式学习允许学习者对未标记样本的标签进行查询访问，有潜力显著降低标签复杂度。本文旨在探索这种交互式学习框架下，是否可以设计出计算高效的算法来提升样本效率，并探索其理论界限。

Method: 提出了一种交互式学习算法，利用黑盒标签查询访问。算法的查询复杂度为$d \cdot polylog(1/ε) + \tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$，其中$p$是目标函数的偏置。通过分析算法在计算上的高效性，并证明了其查询复杂度在质量上接近最优。此外，还通过下界定理，展示了基于池的主动学习中任何学习器需要$\tilde{\Omega}(d/ε)$标签，除非抽取超多项式数量的未标记样本。

Result: 成功设计了首个计算高效的交互式学习者，其误差为$O(opt) + ε$。证明了该算法的查询复杂度界限在质量上接近最优。此外，还证明了查询访问对于改善被动学习的标签复杂度是必不可少的，且对于池基主动学习，任何学习器除非抽取超多项式数量的未标记样本，否则需要$\tilde{\Omega}(d/ε)$标签。

Conclusion: 本文填补了交互式学习中ReLU学习的空白，提供了高效的算法和紧密的理论界限，强调了查询访问在降低标签复杂度中的重要性。这些发现为未来的研究提供了坚实的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: We study the task of agnostically learning general (as opposed to homogeneous) ReLUs under the Gaussian distribution with respect to the squared loss. In the passive learning setting, recent work gave a computationally efficient algorithm that uses $poly(d,1/ε)$ labeled examples and outputs a hypothesis with error $O(opt)+ε$, where $opt$ is the squared loss of the best fit ReLU. Here we focus on the interactive setting, where the learner has some form of query access to the labels of unlabeled examples. Our main result is the first computationally efficient learner that uses $d polylog(1/ε)+\tilde{O}(\min\{1/p, 1/ε\})$ black-box label queries, where $p$ is the bias of the target function, and achieves error $O(opt)+ε$. We complement our algorithmic result by showing that its query complexity bound is qualitatively near-optimal, even ignoring computational constraints. Finally, we establish that query access is essentially necessary to improve on the label complexity of passive learning. Specifically, for pool-based active learning, any active learner requires $\tildeΩ(d/ε)$ labels, unless it draws a super-polynomial number of unlabeled examples.

</details>


### [248] [First-Order Trajectory Matching: Fast Ensemble Predictions of Chaotic, Turbulent, Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11138)
*Shreya Jha,Timo Schorlepp,Nicholas Geissler,Jules Berman,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一阶轨迹匹配(FTM)方法，通过匹配轨迹的一阶运动直接从随机系统轨迹中学习概率流速度，无需估计漂移、扩散和分数，即可在低计算成本下实现轨迹感知的集合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机系统建模方法通常需要估计漂移、扩散或分数函数，计算复杂且不稳定。本文旨在开发一种直接从轨迹学习概率流速度的方法，既能保持时间边缘分布以匹配集合平均，又能捕捉通量、环流和穿越势垒电流等轨迹特性。

Method: 提出一阶轨迹匹配(FTM)方法：通过匹配轨迹的对称一阶运动来学习概率流速度；直接从轨迹数据学习，避免漂移、扩散和分数估计；建立稳定性分析框架，将离散化误差与采样方差分离，证明在时间分辨率和样本量适当平衡时，单步无仿真FTM损失是稳定的。

Result: 在随机动力系统和偏微分方程示例上的实验表明，FTM能够以低计算成本（确定性推演）提供轨迹感知的集合预测；稳定性分析揭示了时间分辨率与样本量之间的平衡关系；成功捕捉了通量、环流和穿越势垒电流等轨迹相关量。

Conclusion: FTM为随机系统的代理建模提供了新的有效途径，通过直接学习概率流速度，避免了传统方法中复杂的漂移、扩散和分数估计，在保证计算效率的同时实现了轨迹感知的集合预测能力。

Abstract: We introduce First-Order Trajectory Matching (FTM), a surrogate-modeling method that learns the first-order local transport of probability mass from trajectories of stochastic systems. By matching the symmetric first-order motion of trajectories, FTM learns the probability current velocity, whose flow preserves time marginals to match ensemble averages, while also capturing current-like trajectory quantities such as fluxes, circulations, and barrier-crossing currents. FTM learns the current velocity directly from trajectories, avoiding drift, diffusion, and score estimation. Our stability analysis separates discretization error from sampling variance and shows that the one-step simulation-free FTM loss is stable when temporal resolution and sample size are properly balanced. Across stochastic dynamical systems and PDE examples, we empirically demonstrate that FTM provides trajectory-aware ensemble predictions at low, deterministic-rollout cost.

</details>


### [249] [OncoTraj: a public benchmark for longitudinal resistance prediction in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer on osimertinib](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11144)
*Abhijoy Sarkar,Aarchi Singh Thakur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 发布了OncoTraj基准，包含813例EGFR突变NSCLC患者接受一线奥希替尼治疗的纵向数据，定义了三个预测任务，发现单时间点组织NGS数据存在预测上限，需ctDNA数据突破瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGFR突变NSCLC患者对一线奥希替尼的耐药性是克隆演化的典型范例，但缺乏公开基准数据集用于训练和评估计算模型预测患者纵向轨迹，阻碍了该领域方法学发展。

Method: 整合MSK-CHORD、AACR GENIE BPC NSCLC和FLAURA三个临床基因组数据源，构建813例患者的标准化数据集；定义三个锁定任务：12个月进展二分类、进展时间回归、主导耐药机制六分类；提供无泄漏的数据划分和开源评估框架，包含六种基线模型。

Result: 基于单时间点组织NGS特征，所有模型类在纯净跨源评估中均未超越随机水平，表明预测上限源于输入模态而非算法；成功复现文献一致性发现：TP53共突变将12个月进展率从29%提升至59%。

Conclusion: OncoTraj建立了可复现、经泄漏审计的基准，揭示了单时间点组织NGS的预测局限性，为ctDNA增强的v2版本提供了明确的设计需求，指明了突破预测瓶颈的技术路径。

Abstract: Resistance to first-line osimertinib in EGFR-mutant non-small-cell lung cancer (NSCLC) is the canonical example of predictable clonal evolution under therapeutic pressure, yet no public benchmark exists for training or evaluating computational models on the corresponding longitudinal patient trajectories. We introduce OncoTraj, a public benchmark of 813 EGFR-mutant NSCLC patients receiving first-line osimertinib, harmonized from three real-world clinical-genomic sources: MSK-CHORD (672 patients), AACR Project GENIE BPC NSCLC (34 patients), and the FLAURA molecular-resistance supplement (107 patients). OncoTraj defines three locked tasks: (A) binary classification of progression by a fixed 12-month landmark, (B) regression of time-to-first-progression in days, and (C) six-class classification of the dominant resistance mechanism. We release the harmonized dataset, patient-level train/validation/test splits with an audited no-leakage guarantee, an open-source evaluation harness, and six reference baselines spanning a majority-class predictor, logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, an LSTM, and a multi-task transformer. With v1's single-timepoint snapshot features, no task clears chance on clean within-source evaluation: the uniformity of this ceiling across every model class localizes the limit to the input modality (single-snapshot tissue NGS rather than serial ctDNA), not the algorithm. The benchmark does recover a reproducible literature-consistent association: TP53 co-mutation raises the 12-month progression rate from 29% to 59% cohort-wide. OncoTraj establishes a reproducible, leakage-audited baseline and converts the modality limit into concrete design requirements for a serial-ctDNA-enriched v2.

</details>


### [250] [Efficiently Learning Drifting Halfspaces with Massart Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11149)
*Mingchen Ma,Guyang Cao,Jelena Diakonikolas,Ilias Diakonikolas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在Massart噪声和概念漂移环境下的在线学习问题，针对边际可分线性分类器提出了高效算法，错误率达到$\eta + \tilde{O}(\Delta^{1/3}/\gamma)$，并证明$\Delta^{1/3}$的依赖在低阶多项式测试下是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在现实世界中常见的概念漂移和非随机噪声同时存在的学习场景，旨在理解这种复杂设置下学习的计算复杂性，特别是信息论最优与计算可行性之间的差距。

Method: 针对边际可分线性分类器，设计了计算高效的在线学习算法，算法考虑了Massart噪声率$\eta$、漂移率$\Delta$和边际参数$\gamma$；在可实现设置下，通过技术改进获得了比先前工作更好的错误率；在理论分析上，使用低阶多项式测试框架证明下界。

Result: 提出了错误率为$\eta + \tilde{O}(\Delta^{1/3}/\gamma)$的高效学习算法；在可实现设置下获得了优于先前工作的错误率；证明了信息论最优错误率与计算可行性错误率之间的差距：信息论最优为$\Delta^{1/2}$量级，但低阶多项式测试无法突破$\Delta^{1/3}$量级。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了漂移概念学习中信息与计算复杂性的根本性折衷：虽然信息论上可以达到$\Delta^{1/2}$的错误率，但在计算受限情况下$\Delta^{1/3}$的错误率是不可避免的，表明算法性能在本质上是最优的。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning a drifting concept in the presence of Massart noise. In this framework, an online learner has access to a history of independent samples whose labels are noisy versions of a target concept that may change from round to round. The goal is to output, in each round, a hypothesis with small prediction error. We study the complexity of this learning problem for the fundamental class of margin-separable linear classifiers (halfspaces). On the positive side, we give a computationally efficient learner achieving error $η+ \tilde O(Δ^{1/3}/γ)$, where $η$ upper bounds the Massart noise rate, $Δ$ is the drift rate, and $γ$ is the margin. Interestingly, in the realizable setting, an adaptation of our techniques yields an efficient learner with an improved error rate over prior work. On the lower-bound side, we provide formal evidence of an information-computation tradeoff, strongly suggesting that our algorithm's performance is essentially optimal. Specifically, while the information-theoretically optimal error scales with $Δ^{1/2}$, we prove that $Δ^{1/3}$-scaling is unavoidable for low-degree polynomial tests, even in the special case of random classification noise.

</details>


### [251] [COGENT: Continuous Graph Emulators with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for Long-Term Physical Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11162)
*Zesheng Liu,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了COGENT，一个基于神经常微分方程的连续图模拟器，用于非规则地理空间网格上的长期物理预测，能够生成任意未来时刻的预测并提升长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于网格的物理模拟方法通常受限于固定的时间离散化，且在长期预测中存在稳定性问题。对于非规则地理空间网格上的物理预测，需要一个能够处理任意时间查询、保持长期稳定性的连续时间预测框架。

Method: COGENT采用图神经网络编码器处理有限历史状态和外强迫场，生成节点级上下文向量；利用神经常微分方程(Neural ODE)建模连续潜在动力学系统，通过插值的未来强迫场和相对预测时间驱动；使用残差解码器将潜在轨迹映射回物理状态，实现无需自回归的多步预测；并提出预测时域采样和渐进式预测时域调度策略以稳定长期训练。

Result: 在冰盖和海平面系统模型生成的瞬态冰盖模拟数据集上进行评估，COGENT相比自回归图神经网络基线模型展现出更好的长期稳定性，能够有效进行长期物理预测。

Conclusion: 连续图神经常微分方程为非规则地理空间网格上的可扩展物理预测提供了一种有前景的方法，特别适用于需要稳定长期预测和任意时间查询能力的应用场景。

Abstract: In this work, we present COGENT, a continuous graph emulator with Neural Ordinary Differential Equations for long-term physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes. COGENT encodes a finite history of system states and associated forcing fields and external forcings with a graph-based history encoder, producing node-wise context vectors that capture both local spatial interactions and temporal evolution. These context vectors initialize and condition a latent Neural Ordinary Differential Equation whose dynamics are driven by interpolated future forcings and explicit relative rollout time. By modeling the forecast trajectory as a continuous latent dynamical system, COGENT can generate predictions at arbitrary future times rather than being restricted to a fixed temporal discretization. A residual decoder maps the resulting latent trajectories back to future physical states, enabling direct multi-step forecasting without repeatedly feeding predicted states back into the model. This formulation combines graph-based spatial representation, history-conditioned latent dynamics, and continuous-time rollout in a unified framework for mesh-based physical simulation emulation. In order to stabilize training with long-horizon supervision, we also propose effective rollout-horizon sampling and a progressive rollout-horizon scheduling strategy. We evaluate COGENT on transient ice-sheet simulations generated by the Ice-sheet and Sea-level System Model, demonstrating improved long-range stability over autoregressive graph baselines. These results suggest that continuous graph Neural ODEs provide a promising methodology for scalable physical forecasting on irregular geospatial meshes, particularly in applications that require stable long-horizon predictions and the ability to query system states at arbitrary times.

</details>


### [252] [Algorithmic and Minimax Complexities in Kernel Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11171)
*Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在频率学派RKHS老虎机框架下统一了GP-UCB与DEC两种理论视角，提出融合两者优势的防护型主算法，证明算法信息与类别级极小化最优系数适用于不同问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-UCB与决策估计系数（DEC）方法表面上分属不同理论体系，缺乏统一理解；需要建立共同框架阐明两者关系并发挥各自优势。

Method: 提出统一MAIR框架，引入异构半正定算法先验，推广GP-UCB分析，构建防护型主算法结合GP-UCB与MAMS优势。

Result: 成功统一两种理论视角，在核老虎机设置中证明算法复杂度在过参数化模型中比类别级极小化最优或DEC证书更具信息性，算法信息与类别级极小化系数导致不同间隙。

Conclusion: 算法信息与类别级极小化最优系数回答不同问题并可产生不同结果，核老虎机提供了清晰展示这一区别的数学框架。

Abstract: Gaussian-process upper confidence bound (GP-UCB) and decision-estimation-coefficient (DEC) methods may appear, at first sight, to belong to different theories. This paper places the two viewpoints in a common algorithmic-information language for frequentist RKHS bandits. GP-UCB fixes an algorithmic, rather than true, Gaussian-process prior and exploits realized-trajectory complexity together with computational tractability, whereas MAMS optimizes a robust class-wide MAIR/DEC envelope. Through the unified MAIR framework and heterogeneous positive-semidefinite algorithmic priors, we generalize both the GP-UCB analysis and the MAMS algorithm, propose a safeguarded master that combines their advantages, and provide a kernel-bandit construction showing that algorithmic complexity can be more informative than class-wide minimax or DEC certificates in overparameterized models. The resulting message is that algorithmic information and class-wide minimax coefficients answer different questions and can lead to different gaps; kernel bandits provide a clean setting in which this distinction becomes mathematically visible.

</details>


### [253] [Predicting Future Behaviors in Reasoning Models Enables Better Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11172)
*Evgenii Kortukov,Piotr Komorowski,Florian Klein,Paula Engl,Gabriele Sarti,Seong Joon Oh,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文区分了“检测特征”和“预测特征”，发现前者难以有效引导大推理模型（LRM）的未来行为；提出FPCG方法，利用预测特征进行文本级引导，在几乎不降低输出质量的情况下实现对模型行为的有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大推理模型（LRM）在部署时常表现出不可预期的行为。尽管测试时引导可以通过干预模型的隐藏表示来控制输出，但往往会降低输出质量。作者指出，现有的引导方法隐式依赖于“检测特征”（即检测已生成文本中行为的特征），但这些特征对未来的行为结果预测能力较差，因此并非理想的干预目标。这促使作者探索能够预测未来行为的内部特征，并基于此设计更有效的引导方法。

Method: 作者首先训练激活探针，从中间推理步骤预测未来行为的可能性，这些探针在预测最可能行为时达到了64%-91%的准确率，揭示了“预测特征”的存在。基于这些预测特征，作者提出了Future Probe Controlled Generation (FPCG)方法：该方法采样多个候选句子，并利用预测未来行为可能性的探针选择最佳候选句子，从而实现文本级的引导。

Result: 实验结果表明，FPCG方法能够在几乎不降低输出质量的情况下实现有效引导。此外，FPCG在多个评估场景中实现了激活引导无法完成的引导任务，证明了区分检测特征和预测特征对于控制LRM行为的重要性。

Conclusion: 本文通过区分检测特征和预测特征，为控制大推理模型行为提供了更细致的方法论。基于预测特征的FPCG方法能够在保持输出质量的同时，有效引导模型行为，为未来的模型控制和优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Deployed large reasoning models (LRMs) often behave unexpectedly. Test-time steering controls LRM outputs by intervening on their hidden representations, but it can degrade output quality. We argue that prior steering work implicitly relies on internal features that detect behavior in already generated text. We show that these detection features are poor predictors of future behavioral outcomes, and thus not the natural intervention target. Instead, we train activation probes to predict future behavior likelihoods from intermediate reasoning steps. These probes predict the most likely behavior with 64%-91% accuracy, revealing a separate type of internal prediction features. Building on these prediction features, we introduce a text-level steering method, Future Probe Controlled Generation. FPCG samples multiple candidate sentences and chooses the best one according to a probe predicting the future behavior likelihood. This enables steering with almost no output quality degradation. FPCG also enables steering in several evaluations where activation steering fails. These results show that distinguishing detection and prediction features enables a more nuanced approach to controlling LRM behaviors.

</details>


### [254] [When to Align, When to Predict: A Phase Diagram for Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11190)
*Ilay Kamai,Hugues Van Assel,Aviv Regev,Hagai B. Perets,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了统一线性框架，揭示了跨模态对齐（CA）和跨模态预测（CP）的互补失效模式，并提出数据驱动方法在训练前诊断多模态问题并选择正确目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态对齐和跨模态预测是多模态表示学习的主流范式，但缺乏系统理解：何时成功、何时失败、何时跨模态训练有效。这导致科学领域（如生物医学、天体物理学）从业者无法诊断标准方法为何表现不如单模态。

Method: 提出统一线性框架，基于带结构化跨模态干扰相关的spike信号加噪声模型，推导两种目标的分离比。开发数据驱动程序，使用少量标注样本将现实数据集定位在四区域相图中，在跨模态训练前识别首选目标和预测方向。

Result: 揭示了互补失效模式：对齐对每个模态进行白化，当干扰在视图间强相关时失效；预测通过单侧白化编码跨可预测内容，恢复由源模态质量决定。实验在合成数据、立体视觉基准、图像-标题对和真实天体物理数据上验证了非线性区域的预测，包括跨模态训练有害的Neither区域。

Conclusion: 本文框架让从业者在投入训练前诊断多模态问题并选择正确目标，为多模态学习提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment (CA) and cross-modal prediction (CP) are the dominant paradigms for multimodal representation learning, yet there is no systematic understanding of when each succeeds, when each fails, and when cross-modal training helps at all -- a gap that leaves practitioners, especially in scientific domains like biomedicine or astrophysics, with heterogeneous instruments and multiple levels of organization and measurement, unable to diagnose why standard methods underperform the best single modality. We develop a unified linear framework that addresses both questions. Under a spiked signal-plus-noise model with structured cross-modal nuisance correlation, we derive separation ratios for both objectives that expose complementary failure modes: alignment whitens each modality and fails when nuisance is strongly correlated across views; prediction encodes whatever is cross-predictable through a one-sided whitening, with recovery governed by source-modality quality. The resulting phase diagram partitions multimodal problems into four regimes: Both, CA only, CP only, and Neither. We present a data-driven procedure to locate real-world datasets in this diagram using a small labeled subsample, identifying the preferred objective and prediction direction before any cross-modal training. Experiments on synthetic data, stereo-vision benchmarks, image-caption pairs, and real astrophysical data validate the predictions in the nonlinear regime, including the Neither regime where cross-modal training is actively harmful. Our framework lets practitioners diagnose their multimodal problem and choose the right objective before committing to training. Code to reproduce the results is available at https://github.com/IlayMalinyak/mm_align_vs_pred.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [EstRTL: Functional Estimation Guided RTL Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09867)
*Qi Xiong,Renzhi Chen,Bowei Wang,Yuqing Xiong,Libo Huang,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出EstRTL框架，通过静态功能分数估计的三阶段流程（生成-估计-纠正），提升LLM生成RTL代码的功能正确性，准确率提升3.2%-9.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成RTL代码的方法主要关注模型微调和扩展技术，缺乏对功能正确性的关注，无法确保生成的代码在真实硬件实现中按预期工作。

Method: 提出EstRTL，一个基于LLM的协作代理框架，采用三阶段范式：生成、估计和纠正。功能估计代理基于分数和评估结果静态评估生成的代码，决定直接输出、返回重生成或转发给代码纠正代理。

Result: 实验结果表明，EstRTL将通用LLM生成RTL代码的正确性显著提升了3.2%-9.0%，证明了系统的实用价值。

Conclusion: EstRTL框架可以应用于各种设计用于RTL代码生成的LLM，通过提供定量分数和人类可读的需求比较，提高了AI辅助RTL代码生成的透明度和正确性。

Abstract: Optimizing register transfer level (RTL) code is of vital importance in hardware design. Large language models (LLMs) provide new methods for the automatic generation and optimization of RTL code, offering the potential to significantly accelerate the design process and reduce human effort. However, existing methods for generating RTL code often focus on model fine-tuning and the use of various expansion techniques to enhance the RTL code generation capabilities, lacking attention to the functional correctness. Ensuring that the generated RTL code not only compiles successfully but also behaves as intended in real hardware implementations remains a critical challenge. To address this issue, we propose EstRTL, an LLM-powered collaborative agent framework for RTL code generation based on static functional score estimation. EstRTL operates a three-stage paradigm: Generation, Estimation and Correction. During the stages, the functional estimation agent statically evaluates the generated code based on score and assessment results, and decides whether to output the code directly, return it for regeneration, or forward it to the code correction agent. This framework can be applied to various LLMs that designed for RTL code generation, further enhancing the correctness of the generated code. By providing quantitative scores and human-readable requirements comparisons, it improves the transparency of AI-assisted RTL code generation. Experiments show that EstRTL significantly improves the correctness of RTL code generation by generic LLM by 3.2\%-9.0\%, demonstrating the practical value of our system. The codes and experimental results are open-sourced at link: https://anonymous.4open.science/status/EstRTL-E200/.

</details>


### [256] [Toward a Small ML Runtime Stack for Raspberry Pi 5 QPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09905)
*Yiannis Hadjiyianni,Panagiotis Michelakis,Dimitrios Stamoulis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向树莓派5 VideoCore VII QPU的机器学习运行时栈，通过优化的整数运算实现相比NumPy近两个数量级的吞吐量提升，证明了边缘设备QPU加速AI推理的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘AI需求增长，现有树莓派5的VideoCore VII QPU计算能力未被充分利用。研究者希望构建专用运行时栈，将QPU作为实用的AI模型执行加速底层，提升边缘设备上的AI推理性能。

Method: 基于py-videocore7汇编库构建QPU优先的ML运行时栈，包含可复用的分块矩阵乘法基板、GEMM支持的卷积、单头注意力核心、持久执行器和基于smul24指令的整数执行单元，采用打包INT16输入与INT32累加策略优化整数核性能。

Result: 密集整数核实现相比NumPy近两个数量级（100倍）的吞吐量提升；在min/max、池化、卷积、注意力等操作上，性能均优于PyTorch和NumPy基准。

Conclusion: 树莓派QPU可作为边缘AI模型执行加速的实用底层，为低功耗设备上的机器学习推理提供了可行的高性能替代方案。

Abstract: We present a QPU-first ML runtime stack for Raspberry Pi 5's VideoCore VII QPU, built on top of the py-videocore7 assembly library. The system comprises reusable tiled matrix-multiplication substrate, GEMM-backed convolution, a single-head attention-style core, persistent executors, and integer execution based on smul24 instructions. For dense integer kernels, packed INT16-input with INT32 accumulation achieves nearly two orders of magnitude higher throughput over NumPy. Across operations (min/max, pooling, convolution, attention), we report improved performance over both PyTorch and NumPy. Our preliminary results indicate that Raspberry QPUs can serve as a practical execution substrate towards accelerating AI model execution at the edge.

</details>


### [257] [ARTA: Adaptive Reinforcement-Learning-Based Throttling Agent for RowHammer Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09915)
*Marco Ho,Michael S. Hsiao,Jeeho Ryoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ARTA，一种基于强化学习的轻量级节流机制，通过监控DRAM刷新窗口内的细粒度内存访问行为并动态调整核心吞吐量，有效缓解RowHammer攻击，无需DRAM端硬件修改或离线训练，在低激活阈值下消除所有位翻转并显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DRAM工艺缩放，RowHammer漏洞日益严重，激活阈值降低导致现有的防御机制（如TRR、ECC和基于刷新的机制）容易受到复杂的多存储体锤击模式攻击，亟需一种无需硬件修改、适应性强且高效的缓解方案。

Method: 提出ARTA机制，在内存控制器中使用小型SRAM结构（每核心每存储体的CBF FIFO队列和紧凑Q表），通过Q学习频率缩放调节器监控t_REFW窗口内的细粒度内存访问行为，动态调整核心吞吐量以检测和抑制RowHammer活动，无需DRAM端硬件修改或离线训练。

Result: ARTA在N_BO低至64时消除所有位翻转，在N_BO为20时将位翻转减少高达22K倍，并通过限制预防性操作开销使性能相比最先进缓解机制提升高达73.6%，改善了内存带宽吞吐量。

Conclusion: 基于自适应强化学习的节流机制为新兴DRAM系统提供了稳健、可扩展且高性能的RowHammer缓解方案，证明了无需硬件修改的软件方法在应对RowHammer漏洞方面的有效性。

Abstract: RowHammer vulnerability continues to intensify with DRAM scaling, reducing the activation threshold needed to induce bitflips and rendering existing defenses such as TRR, ECC, and refresh-based mechanisms vulnerable to sophisticated multi-bank hammering patterns. This work presents ARTA, a lightweight reinforcement-learning-based throttling mechanism that detects and suppresses RowHammer activity by monitoring fine-grained memory access behavior within the DRAM refresh window (t_REFW) and dynamically adjusting core throughput using a Q-learning frequency scaling governor. ARTA requires no DRAM-side hardware modification or offline training, using small SRAM structures in the memory controller -- a per-core, per-bank FIFO queue (CBF) and a compact Q-table -- for immediate deployment. Our evaluation shows that ARTA eliminates all bitflips at N_BO values down to 64, reduces bitflips up to 22K times at N_BO of 20, and improves performance up to 73.6% over state-of-the-art mitigation mechanisms by limiting preventive action overheads for improved memory bandwidth throughput. These results demonstrate that adaptive RL-based throttling provides robust, scalable, and high-performance RowHammer mitigation for emerging DRAM systems.

</details>


### [258] [SPARX: Secure and Privacy-Aware Approximate CNN Acceleration with Edge RISC-V SoC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09946)
*Sonu Kumar,Akash Sankhe,Mukul Lokhande,Santosh Kumar Vishvakarma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARX框架，在RISC-V SoC上集成近似CNN加速、隐私保护和安全认证，通过系统化指标选择ILM近似乘法器，实现了面积减少51.7%、功耗降低81.5%、吞吐量提升2.13倍，仅需精度损失2.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI系统在严格能耗、性能、安全和隐私约束下需要实时CNN推理。现有近似CNN加速器大多未同时考虑安全与隐私感知的边缘部署问题，缺乏兼顾效率、安全性和隐私保护的统一解决方案。

Method: 提出SPARX框架，包含：1）定制RISC-V指令扩展；2）近似对数CNN加速单元；3）轻量级差分噪声隐私引擎；4）挑战-响应认证机制。引入近似感知决策框架，采用ASI、AE、QoA、AFOM和HAE五个指标系统评估11种近似MAC架构，选择最优的迭代对数乘法器(ILM)设计。

Result: ILM相比精确radix-4 Booth MAC实现：面积减少51.7%，功耗降低81.5%，吞吐量提升2.13倍，ResNet-20/CIFAR-10精度仅下降2.82个百分点。FPGA实现(Xilinx VC707)在250MHz下达到58.4 GOPS/W能效，28nm CMOS物理实现验证了ASIC可行性。

Conclusion: SPARX成功实现了安全、隐私感知的近似CNN加速，在保持可接受精度损失的前提下显著提升了硬件效率，为边缘AI系统提供了集成安全、隐私和效率的综合解决方案。

Abstract: Edge-AI systems increasingly require real-time CNN inference under strict energy, performance, security, and privacy constraints. Approximate computing improves hardware efficiency by exploiting the error resilience of neural network workloads; however, most approximate CNN accelerators do not jointly consider secure, privacy-aware edge deployment. This paper presents SPARX, a Secure and Privacy-Aware Approximate CNN Acceleration framework integrated within a heterogeneous RV32IMC RISC-V System-on-Chip (SoC). SPARX combines a custom RISC-V instruction extension, an approximate logarithmic CNN acceleration unit, a lightweight differential-noise-based privacy engine, and a challenge-response authentication mechanism. To guide arithmetic selection, an approximation-aware decision framework is introduced that uses the Approximation Severity Index (ASI), Approximation Efficiency (AE), Quality of Approximation (QoA), Approximation Figure-of-Merit (AFOM), and Hardware Acceleration Efficiency (HAE). Evaluation across 11 state-of-the-art approximate MAC architectures identifies the Iterative Logarithmic Multiplier (ILM) as the most suitable design, achieving 51.7% area reduction, 81.5% power reduction, and 2.13x throughput improvement compared with an accurate radix-4 Booth MAC, while only reducing ResNet-20/CIFAR-10 accuracy by 2.82 percentage points. FPGA implementation on a Xilinx VC707 platform achieves 58.4 GOPS/W energy efficiency at 250 MHz, while 28-nm CMOS physical implementation validates ASIC feasibility

</details>


### [259] [Toward Intelligent Prefetching: A Survey on Complex Memory Access Prediction Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09955)
*Sheel Sindhu Manohar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了机器学习在数据预取中的应用，提出了涵盖局部性类型、实现层级和学习范式的分类体系，并通过多维度对比分析揭示了准确率与开销的权衡关系、模型复杂度与缓存层级的映射，以及运行时适应性与模型容量之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于下一行、步长和相关性启发式的预取器对于规则访存模式表现良好，但在现代工作负载（如图分析、稀疏矩阵计算和指针密集型应用）中普遍存在的不规则、数据依赖访问模式下表现不足，这驱动了对更先进预取技术的研究需求。

Method: 采用PRISMA指导的选择方法论进行系统性文献综述，提出了一个结构化分类体系，从三个维度组织预取技术：局部性类型（空间和时间局部性）、实现层级（硬件、软件和混合方法），以及针对ML预取器的学习范式（监督、强化和无监督学习）与训练模式（在线和离线训练）。

Result: 通过对ML预取器在存储开销、准确率、推理延迟、硬件可行性和泛化能力等多维度的对比分析，识别出三个关键发现：由模型类别定义的准确率-开销帕累托前沿、模型复杂度与缓存层级之间的自然架构映射，以及运行时适应性与模型容量之间的根本张力。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了机器学习预取技术中的关键权衡关系，这些发现为分层集成架构的设计提供了理论基础，指明了未来预取器设计应平衡准确率、开销和适应性的发展方向。

Abstract: Data prefetching is a critical technique for bridging the processor-memory performance gap by predicting future memory accesses and retrieving data into on-chip caches before demand. While traditional prefetchers based on next-line, stride, and correlation heuristics perform well for regular access patterns, they are fundamentally inadequate for the irregular, data-dependent patterns prevalent in modern workloads such as graph analytics, sparse matrix computations, and pointer-intensive applications. This survey presents a systematic review of papers using a PRISMA-guided selection methodology. We propose a structured taxonomy that organizes prefetching techniques across three dimensions: locality type, including spatial and temporal locality; implementation layer, including hardware, software, and hybrid approaches; and, for the increasingly important class of ML-based prefetchers, learning paradigm, including supervised, reinforcement, and unsupervised learning, paired with training mode, including online and offline training. Through a multi-dimensional comparative analysis of ML-based prefetchers evaluated across storage overhead, accuracy, inference latency, hardware feasibility, and generalization ability, we identify three key findings: an accuracy-overhead Pareto frontier defined by model class, a natural architectural mapping between model complexity and cache hierarchy level, and a fundamental tension between runtime adaptability and model capacity that motivates hierarchical ensemble architectures.

</details>


### [260] [A Generic Modulo-$(2^n\pmδ)$ RNS Multiplier Based on Twit Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09965)
*Saeid Gorgin,Amirhossein Sadr,Behzad Salami,Dara Rahmati*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于twit表示的通用模$(2^n \pm δ)$乘法器，通过延迟进位传播和优化架构，在45nm工艺下实现了延迟降低20.5%、面积减少13.2%、功耗降低28.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模乘法是余数系统(RNS)中的基本运算，在密码学、信号处理和机器学习加速器中是延迟、面积和能耗的主要来源。虽然近期工作提出了适用于模$2^n \pm δ$的twit-based余数表示，并实现了高效的模加法和减法，但与该表示兼容的高效模乘法器一直缺失。

Method: 提出了一种通用的twit-based模$(2^n \pm δ)$乘法器架构，包括：操作数分割、模部分积生成、进位保存累加、溢出折叠以及twit兼容的最终模加法。关键创新是将进位传播延迟到最终阶段，避免了传统"先乘后约"设计中的长关键路径。使用FreePDK 45nm工艺流程进行实现和综合。

Result: 实验结果表明：平均延迟减少20.5%，面积减少13.2%，功耗降低28.0%。系统级研究显示，这些电路级改进在广泛的模乘法和加法工作负载中转化为更低的端到端延迟。研究涵盖了5位、8位和11位余数通道配置。

Conclusion: 所提出的twit-based模乘法器成功填补了高效模运算的空白，为RNS数据通路提供了显著的性能改进，证明了延迟进位传播策略的有效性，对密码学和信号处理应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Modular multiplication is a fundamental arithmetic primitive in Residue Number Systems (RNS) and is often the dominant source of delay, area, and energy consumption in RNS datapaths used in cryptography, signal processing, and machine-learning accelerators. Recent work introduced a twit-based residue representation for moduli of the form $2^n \pm δ$, with $0 \le δ\le 2^{n-1}-1$, and showed that it enables efficient generic modular addition and subtraction across the full admissible $δ$ range. However, an efficient modular multiplier compatible with the same representation has remained unavailable. This paper presents a generic twit-based modulo-$(2^n \pm δ)$ multiplier for RNS channels. The proposed architecture computes the product through operand splitting, modular partial-product generation, carry-save accumulation, overflow folding, and a twit-compatible final modular addition. By deferring carry propagation to the final stage, the resulting organization avoids the long critical paths characteristic of conventional multiply-then-reduce designs. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, we study a modulus set with 5-bit residue channels and show that, owing to the broad admissible range of $δ$, it can provide a sufficiently wide dynamic range. Moreover, additional 8-bit and 11-bit configurations are used to evaluate the proposed approach at larger channel widths. We implement and synthesize the proposed multiplier in a FreePDK 45\,nm flow, and the results show average reductions of 20.5\% in delay, 13.2\% in area, and 28.0\% in power relative to baseline designs. A system-level study further indicates that these circuit-level improvements translate into lower end-to-end latency over a broad range of modular multiplication and addition workloads.

</details>


### [261] [Fault Characterization and Hardening of Combinational Standard Cells Using 3D-TCAD Simulations for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10015)
*Ali Zarei,Amir M. Hajisadeghi,Hamid R. Zarandi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D-TCAD仿真的方法，用于特征化和增强组合标准单元的抗故障能力，并提出了一种加固版通用NAND单元以提升抗粒子故障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着信息物理系统在关键任务中的应用增加以及技术节点的进步，数字系统组件的可靠性面临严峻挑战，亟需提升组合标准单元的容错能力。

Method: 采用3D-TCAD仿真技术，对广泛使用的标准单元在不同故障能量、粒子角度和相邻单元影响等场景下的故障敏感区域进行高精度特征化分析，并提出加固版NAND单元。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的加固版NAND单元在抗粒子引发故障方面取得了显著改善。

Conclusion: 通过高精度仿真与针对性设计，可以有效提升标准单元的容错能力，为提升信息物理系统的可靠性提供了可行方法。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPSs) are increasingly employed in applications with various levels of mission criticality, making the reliability of digital system components essential for maintaining service quality. On the other hand, advancements in technology nodes have heightened reliability concerns in these systems. This paper presents a method for characterizing and enhancing the fault tolerance of combinational standard cells using 3D-TCAD simulations. Through detailed simulations, we identify fault-sensitive regions in widely used standard cells under diverse scenarios that include variations in fault energy, particle angle, and the adjacency effect of identical and non-identical neighboring cells. Following this high-precision characterization, a hardened version of a universal logic NAND cell is proposed that mitigates its vulnerabilities. Simulation results demonstrate substantial improvements in resilience to particle-induced faults.

</details>


### [262] [Isolation-aware Scheduling Framework for DNN-based End-to-End Autonomous Driving System on Tile-based Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10303)
*Chenguang Zhang,Yuanpeng Zhang,Chenhao Xue,Yihan Yin,Chen Zhang,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-Tile调度框架，通过结合可配置隔离与弹性预留策略，在基于瓦片(tile)的加速器上优化L4+自动驾驶系统的深度神经网络部署，在满足端到端时延约束的同时显著提升资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L4+自动驾驶系统需要在严格时延约束(≤100ms)下运行数十个异构深度神经网络，执行时间变化高达3.3倍。量产成本限制无法为每个功能分配独立硬件，DNN必须密集部署在单芯片上导致共享资源竞争。现有基于预留的调度器固定并行度而浪费灵活性，工作保守调度器假设重分配成本低且忽略任务间依赖，都无法有效利用瓦片加速器的可调并行度和硬件原生隔离特性。

Method: ADS-Tile提出时空隔离-共享空间框架：(1)结合可配置隔离与弹性预留，界定重分配发生的时间与位置；(2)建立概率时延模型，考虑任务速率10-240Hz下的停顿-迁移-重启开销累积效应；(3)设计DAG感知的运行时调度器，在共享端到端时延约束下协同决策任务同置策略与并行度分配。该框架将重分配开销从链式累积转变为可控范围内的有限发生。

Result: 在工业界与学术界联合提出的自动驾驶基准测试上，ADS-Tile在关键时延场景下比工作保守基线减少最多32%的瓦片使用量；将重分配导致的处理能力浪费从17%-44%降至1.2%以下。可控时空共享显著提升了基于瓦片架构自动驾驶系统的资源效率与时延可预测性。

Conclusion: ADS-Tile证明了在瓦片加速器上通过时空隔离-共享空间可以有效平衡隔离性与灵活性，为自动驾驶系统提供了既满足严格时延约束又高效利用硬件资源的调度解决方案，对量产自动驾驶系统的DNN部署具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: Level-4+ autonomous driving systems (ADS) must run dozens of heterogeneous deep neural networks (DNNs) as end-to-end (E2E) pipelines under a strict latency constraint (<=100 ms), even as execution time varies by up to 3.3x. Cost rules out dedicating isolated hardware to each function in mass-produced ADS, so these DNNs must be densely colocated on a single chip, which introduces shared-resource contention. Tile-based accelerators expose two scheduling opportunities that conventional ADS schedulers do not exploit. First, they provide a tunable degree of parallelism (DoP): assigning more tiles raises DoP and can shorten DNN execution time. Second, they provide hardware-native isolation: tiles can be physically partitioned among co-located DNNs. But using this flexibility is expensive: changing a task's DoP triggers a stop-migrate-restart reallocation of its weights and intermediate features. At ADS task rates of 10-240 Hz, these stalls accumulate along E2E chains and threaten deadlines. Reservation-based schedulers fix DoP and leave this flexibility unused; work-conserving schedulers exploit it but assume reallocation is cheap and treat deadlines as independent. We present ADS-Tile that combines configurable isolation and elastic reservation into a spatio-temporal isolation-sharing space that bounds where and when reallocation occurs; a probabilistic latency model and a DAG-aware runtime scheduler then use this space to decide task colocation and DoP under shared E2E deadlines. On an industry- and academia- derived ADS benchmark, ADS-Tile uses up to 32% fewer tiles than the work-conserving baseline in deadline-critical settings and cuts reallocation-induced wasted processing capacity from 17%-44% to below 1.2%. Controlled spatio-temporal sharing improves resource efficiency and latency predictability for tile-based ADS.

</details>


### [263] [A 185 TOPS/W/mm2 Bayesian Inference Engine with 640 aJ Write-Free FeFET GRNG for Uncertainty-Aware Aerial Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10822)
*Zephan M. Enciso,Xuezhong Niu,Xingtian Wang,Mohammad Mehdi Sharifi,Subhasish Mukherjee,Likai Pei,Halid Mulaosmanovic,Stefan Duenkel,Sven Beyer,Michael Niemier,Kai Ni,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于铁电场效应晶体管（FeFET）的贝叶斯推理引擎，通过嵌入式无写入中心极限定理高斯随机数生成器，实现了能耗降低560倍的边缘AI加速器，显著提升了空中搜救任务中目标检测的不确定性校准与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中搜救任务要求在不确定且快速变化的环境中快速可靠地检测受害者。确定性深度学习模型会产生过度自信的假阳性结果，迫使无人机系统执行昂贵的验证机动，从而降低搜索覆盖率并增加救援延迟。贝叶斯神经网络虽可提供不确定性感知检测，但其采样开销对于电池受限的边缘平台而言极具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种基于FeFET的贝叶斯推理引擎，其计算存取一体化宏单元中嵌入了无写入的中心极限定理高斯随机数生成器（CLT-GRNG）。通过从随机选择的最小尺寸、一次性编程的FeFET子集中求和电流，该架构在推理过程中消除了能耗高且影响耐久性的写入操作，同时保持可扩展的高斯采样能力。

Result: CLT-GRNG每采样仅消耗640阿焦耳，相比先前的贝叶斯神经网络加速器实现了560倍的能效提升；CIM模块达到185 TOPS/W/mm²的运算效率。在空中搜救检测任务中，贝叶斯模型在环境干扰下改善了不确定性校准与鲁棒性，能够过滤低置信度检测结果，减少代价高昂的验证操作。

Conclusion: 该研究成果展示了一种高效节能且具备不确定性感知能力的边缘AI引擎，可为自主搜救系统提供可靠的目标检测能力，有效降低误报带来的风险并提升整体救援效率。

Abstract: Aerial search and rescue missions require fast and reliable victim detection under uncertain and rapidly changing environments. Deterministic deep learning models can produce overconfident false positives, forcing unmanned aircraft systems to perform costly verification maneuvers that reduce search coverage and increase rescue delay. Bayesian neural networks provide uncertainty-aware detection, but their sampling overhead is challenging for battery-constrained edge platforms. This work presents a FeFET-based Bayesian inference engine with a write-free central limit theorem Gaussian random number generator embedded in a compute-in-memory macro. By summing currents from a randomly selected subset of minimum-sized, programmed-once FeFETs, the proposed architecture eliminates energy- and endurance-intensive write operations during inference while maintaining scalable Gaussian sampling. The CLT-GRNG consumes 640 aJ per sample, providing a 560x energy-efficiency improvement over prior BNN accelerators, while the CIM tile achieves 185 TOPS/W/mm2. Evaluated on aerial search and rescue detection, the Bayesian model improves uncertainty calibration and robustness under environmental corruption, reducing risk and enabling low-confidence detections to be filtered before costly verification. These results demonstrate an energy-efficient and uncertainty-aware edge AI engine for autonomous search and rescue systems.

</details>


### [264] [Arithmetic Packing on Wide Integer Datapaths in DSP Primitives of Modern FPGA Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11065)
*Titus Bornträger,Shane Fleming,Philipp Holzinger,Dietmar Fey,Michaela Blott,Thomas B. Preußer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用DSP内部预加法器动态打包任意位宽数据的方法，在FPGA上实现低精度神经网络的高效计算，相比FINN基准减少了21%的LUT使用量，FPS/DSP提升36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络广泛采用低精度量化来降低计算需求，FPGA适合处理异构精度工作负载，但其专用DSP切片仅提供固定宽度的数据通路，在执行低位宽运算时利用率严重不足。现有打包方案要么只支持特定固定位宽，要么在外部支持逻辑上存在资源浪费。

Method: 提出一种利用DSP内部预加法器将多个任意位宽的有符号/无符号输入动态打包到宽乘法器通路的通用方法。基于此，设计了两种架构：一种针对矩阵向量乘法优化，另一种针对卷积优化，并集成到AMD的FINN框架中。

Result: 以UltraNet模型为实验对象，相比FINN基准实现：LUT利用率降低21%，FPS/DSP（每单位DSP的帧率）提升36%。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够高效利用FPGA的DSP资源执行低精度神经网络计算，显著降低逻辑资源消耗并提升计算吞吐量，为异构精度神经网络在FPGA上的部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks increasingly employ low-precision quantization to reduce computational requirements. While FPGAs are well suited for workloads with heterogeneous precisions, their dedicated digital signal processing (DSP) slices only feature fixed-width datapaths that are significantly underutilized by low-bitwidth arithmetic. While previous approaches have already introduced the packing of multiple values onto the same wide DSP datapath, they either only support specific fixed bitwidths or are wasteful regarding the use of additional support logic external to the DSP. This paper proposes an efficient method to dynamically pack multiple (un-)signed inputs with arbitrary bitwidths into a wide multiplier path by leveraging the DSP's internal pre-adder. Building on this, we present two distinct architectures, one optimized for matrix-vector multiplications and the other for convolutions. Our implementations are integrated into AMD's FINN framework. With these optimizations, we reduce the LUT utilization by 21% and increase the FPS/DSP by 36% for the UltraNet model compared to the FINN reference.

</details>


### [265] [Coset Ensemble Decoder for Quantum Error Correction with Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11076)
*Shuang Liang,Jubo Xu,Giulio Bassanino,Qianzhou Wang,Yidong Zhou,Yuncheng Lu,Zhiwen Mo,Paul H. J. Kelly,Bo Yuan,Wayne Luk,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种算法-硬件协同设计的量子纠错解码器，通过陪集集成解码算法和领域专用硬件架构，在电路级去极化噪声模型下实现了比现有MWPM和UF解码器更优的精度-延迟权衡，并将FPGA LUT资源消耗降低至原来的1/8.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模容错量子计算依赖于实时量子纠错（QEC），而解码器作为QEC的关键经典子系统，必须同时实现高逻辑精度和超低延迟。现有解码器（如MWPM和UF）在精度-延迟权衡方面存在局限，且硬件资源随码距增长而线性增加，亟需更优的解决方案。

Method: 算法层面提出陪集集成解码：通过集成森林探索生成多个陪集一致性候选解并聚合，逼近陪集级最大似然解码；采用逆序消除和无损图压缩降低计算与内存复杂度。硬件层面设计领域专用架构，通过时序资源复用避免码距比例资源增长，并提出多体存储哈希和层次化ID映射等优化以缓解高并发访问下的流水线停顿和内存冲突。

Result: 在电路级去极化噪声模型下，该方法实现了比现有MWPM和UF解码器更优的精度-延迟权衡；相比已报道的UF解码器，FPGA LUT消耗降低高达8.2倍；候选数量可调，为不同容错工作负载提供灵活的性能定制能力。

Conclusion: 该算法-硬件协同设计有效改善了量子纠错解码器的精度-延迟权衡与资源效率，为大规模容错量子计算提供了更实用的解码方案；代码已开源（https://github.com/IMSeonL/coset-ensemble-decoder）。

Abstract: Reliable large-scale quantum computation relies on fault-tolerant architectures, where quantum error correction (QEC) continuously extracts and decodes error syndromes in real time. A critical component in QEC is the decoder, a classical subsystem that must simultaneously deliver high logical accuracy and ultra-low latency. This paper presents a novel algorithm-hardware co-design that improves the accuracy-latency trade-off over existing approaches such as vanilla Minimum-Weight Perfect Matching (MWPM) and Union-Find (UF) decoders. At the algorithmic level, we introduce coset ensemble decoding, which improves UF decoding by explicitly exploiting logically equivalent cosets. Our method performs ensemble forest exploration to generate multiple coset-consistent candidates and aggregates them to approximate coset-level maximum-likelihood decoding. We further reduce computational and memory complexity via reverse-order elimination and lossless graph compression, without sacrificing accuracy. At the hardware level, we design a domain-specific architecture that temporally reuses resources, avoiding the code-distance-proportional resource growth in prior spatial architectures. Several optimizations, such as multi-bank memory hashing and hierarchical ID mapping, are proposed to mitigate pipeline stalls and memory conflicts under highly concurrent access patterns. Under a circuit-level depolarizing noise model, our co-design approach achieves a better accuracy-latency trade-off than prior MWPM- and UF-based decoders, while reducing FPGA LUT consumption by up to 8.2 times compared with reported UF-based decoder resources. The tunable candidate number further exposes a flexible design knob, enabling users to tailor decoding performance to the requirements of different fault-tolerant workloads. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/IMSeonL/coset-ensemble-decoder.

</details>


### [266] [Towards Autonomous Accelerator Design: FPGA Accelerator Generation with SECDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11117)
*Vinamra Sharma,Xingjian Fu,Jude Haris,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SECDA-DSE框架将大语言模型集成到FPGA加速器设计流程中，通过推理引导的探索自动生成并优化加速器架构，成功实现了三种加速器的FPGA端到端执行，减少了对人工专业知识的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代人工智能工作负载的FPGA加速器设计需要探索庞大的硬件设计空间，涉及架构参数、数据流策略和内存层次结构，过程极为耗时。现有方法如SECDA虽然支持快速硬件-软件协同设计，但识别高效的加速器配置仍主要依赖手动过程和大量领域知识。

Method: 提出SECDA-DSE框架，将大语言模型集成到SECDA生态系统中。该框架结合了结构化DSE Explorer（用于生成候选架构）和LLM Stack（通过检索增强生成和思维链提示进行推理引导探索），并配有反馈循环以实现迭代式强化优化。

Result: 成功生成了三种加速器设计（逐元素向量乘法、2D卷积和矩阵转置），并在FPGA硬件上完成了端到端执行。生成的设计能够捕捉计算并行性与数据移动之间的内核特定权衡，并能根据不同工作负载适应架构配置。

Conclusion: SECDA-DSE证明了LLM引导的设计空间探索在FPGA加速器设计中的潜力，能够生成符合SECDA规范且可成功综合执行的设计，同时显著减少探索时间和对人工专业知识的依赖。

Abstract: Designing FPGA-based accelerators for modern artificial intelligence workloads requires exploring a large and complex hardware design space that involves architectural parameters, data flow strategies, and memory hierarchies, making the process very time consuming. While existing methodologies such as SECDA enable rapid hardware-software co-design through SystemC simulation and FPGA execution, identifying efficient accelerator configurations remains a largely manual process requiring extensive domain knowledge. SECDA-DSE is a framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) into the SECDA ecosystem to guide design space exploration (DSE) of FPGA-based accelerators. It combines a structured DSE Explorer for generating candidate architectures with an LLM Stack that performs reasoning-guided exploration using retrieval-augmented generation and chain-of-thought prompting, coupled with a feedback loop for iterative and reinforced refinement. Building on our previous work introducing SECDA-DSE, this paper extends its evaluation by generating three accelerator designs, including element-wise vector multiplication, 2D convolution, and matrix transpose, and performing end-to-end execution on FPGA hardware. The results show that SECDA-DSE can generate SECDA-compliant accelerator designs that are successfully synthesized and executed on FPGA hardware. Furthermore, the generated designs capture kernel-specific trade-offs between compute parallelism and data movement, highlighting the potential of LLM-guided exploration to adapt architectural configurations across diverse workloads while reducing exploration time and the need for extensive human expertise.

</details>


### [267] [Defeat the Heap: Zero-Copy Data Movement in AXI4MLIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11158)
*Elam Cohavi,Nicolas Bohm Agostini,Jude Haris,Antonino Tumeo,David Kaeli,José Cano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对AXI4MLIR框架中主机-加速器数据传输的冗余拷贝问题，提出零拷贝优化方案，通过直接在DMA映射内存中分配缓冲区，消除了数据暂存拷贝操作，将主存数据移动减少多达2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着定制硬件加速器在机器学习工作负载中占据核心地位，高效的数据传输对最大化线性代数核的加速器性能至关重要。AXI4MLIR框架在进行主机到加速器的数据传输时，需要将数据从堆分配内存缓冲区拷贝到连续的DMA映射缓冲区，这一过程带来了显著的运行时开销，限制了加速器性能的发挥。

Method: 本文识别出AXI4MLIR中的数据拷贝为冗余的暂存操作，通过扩展accel方言（MLIR dialect）并实现相应的下层支持，使缓冲区直接在DMA映射内存中分配，从而消除主机到加速器传输过程中的暂存拷贝，实现零拷贝数据移动。

Result: 使用可配置的矩阵-矩阵乘法加速器进行评估，实验结果表明，零拷贝优化将主存数据移动减少多达2倍，有效提升了整体加速器利用率。

Conclusion: 该零拷贝优化方法成功消除了AXI4MLIR框架中主机-加速器数据传输的冗余拷贝操作，显著降低了主存数据移动开销，提高了加速器的整体利用率和性能表现。

Abstract: As custom hardware accelerators become increasingly central to machine learning workloads, efficient data transfer is critical for maximizing accelerator performance on linear algebra kernels. AXI4MLIR, an extension of the Multi-Level Intermediate Representation (MLIR) compiler framework for automated generation of host-accelerator driver code, incurs significant runtime overhead due to non-zero-copy CPU-accelerator data movement. During transfers from the host to the accelerator, data is copied from heap-allocated memory buffers into contiguous Direct Memory Access (DMA)-mapped buffers. This work identifies this copy as a redundant staging operation and eliminates it through zero-copy data movement. The optimization extends accel, an MLIR dialect introduced by AXI4MLIR, and implements lowering support that allocates buffers directly within DMA-mapped memory, thereby omitting the staging copy. We evaluate the proposed scheme using a configurable matrix-matrix multiplication accelerator and show that the zero-copy optimization reduces main memory data movement by up to 2x, increasing overall accelerator utilization.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [CAMASA: A CAM-based Dataset from the MASA Living Lab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10641)
*Salvatore Iandolo,Marco Savarese,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Antonio Solida,Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Angelo Porrello,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了CAMASA数据集，这是一个基于基础设施的大规模V2X数据集，包含超过4000万条CAM消息和200万条DENM消息，为轨迹预测、交通仿真器校准和ITS数字孪生开发提供了真实城市环境下的实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹预测基准数据集大多以传感器为中心、地理受限或基于合成的移动轨迹，无法捕捉真实世界的V2X通信动态，缺乏能够反映实际城市交通条件和车联网通信特性的数据集。

Method: 在Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA)收集真实的Cooperative Awareness Messages (CAMs)和Decentralized Environmental Notification Messages (DENMs)，设计了严格的预处理流程，包括数据过滤、针对ETSI隐私驱动的stationID变化的伪名协调，以及将时间归一化为10Hz轨迹以适用于运动预测和时间序列分析。

Result: 构建了CAMASA数据集，包含超过4000万条CAMs和200万条DENMs，重建了超过14000公里的车辆路径，涵盖数万个唯一station ID，为协同智能交通系统(C-ITS)研究提供了统计显著的实证基础。

Conclusion: CAMASA数据集不仅支持轨迹预测研究，还能够校准微观城市交通仿真器（如SUMO），并通过联合建模移动模式和真实部署中的V2X通信覆盖，支持现实的智能交通系统(ITS)数字孪生的开发。

Abstract: Trajectory prediction is a key enabler of autonomous and cooperative driving systems. However, most existing benchmarks are either sensor-centric, geographically constrained, or based on synthetic mobility traces that do not capture real-world V2X communication dynamics. This paper introduces CAMASA, a large-scale infrastructure-based dataset derived from Cooperative Awareness Messages (CAMs) and Decentralized Environmental Notification Messages (DENMs) collected within the Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA). The dataset comprises more than 40 million CAMs and 2 million DENMs recorded under authentic urban traffic conditions over multiple months. We present a rigorous preprocessing pipeline that includes filtering, pseudonym reconciliation to account for ETSI privacy-driven stationID changes, and temporal normalization to 10 Hz trajectories, suitable for motion forecasting and time-series analysis. With over 14,000 km of reconstructed vehicle paths and tens of thousands of unique station IDs, CAMASA provides a statistically significant empirical foundation for research on Cooperative Intelligent Transportation Systems (C-ITS). Beyond trajectory prediction, the dataset enables calibration of microscopic urban traffic simulators (e.g., SUMO) and supports the development of realistic Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) Digital Twins by jointly modeling mobility patterns and V2X communication coverage in real deployments.

</details>


### [269] [High-Speed Generation of Periodic Traffic Patterns on P4TG for DDoS and Burst-Load Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10708)
*Fabian Ihle,Etienne Zink,Michael Menth*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究为开源硬件加速流量生成器P4TG提出了数据平面机制，能够生成周期性时变流量模式（如DDoS攻击和突发负载），解决了现有工具难以模拟真实网络时间变化行为的限制，实现了高达4 Tbit/s的流量整形能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流量生成器（包括P4TG）主要产生恒定比特率流量，无法反映真实网络中观察到的高度时间变化行为，如闪众和微突发。这些模式难以用现有工具在大规模上进行模拟，而准确模拟此类流量模式对评估网络系统的鲁棒性和性能至关重要。

Method: 提出了一种用于P4TG的数据平面机制，能够对周期性时变流量模式进行整形，包括代表DDoS攻击和突发负载场景的模式。该机制可在高达4 Tbit/s的聚合吞吐量下应用于生成的流量。研究评估了不同采样分辨率和周期下的模式准确性并分析了可扩展性。

Result: 该机制成功实现了在高达4 Tbit/s吞吐量下对周期性时变流量模式的整形。评估显示模式准确性在不同采样分辨率和周期下表现良好。实际用例验证包括零丢包吞吐量测定和缓冲区容量测量。此外，展示了基于微突发的攻击场景，能够使UDP接收器过载、交换机缓冲区溢出，并在共享链路上降低TCP吞吐量，同时对传统速率监测保持不可检测。

Conclusion: 该研究成功扩展了P4TG的功能，使其能够生成反映真实网络行为的时间变化流量模式，为网络系统评估提供了更真实的测试环境。该方法在实际应用中证明了其价值，能够揭示传统监测方法无法检测的攻击场景和性能问题。

Abstract: Traffic generators are essential tools for evaluating the robustness and performance of networked systems. P4TG is an open-source, hardware-accelerated traffic generator implemented in P4 for the Intel Tofino ASIC. It has been adopted by researchers and industry due to its flexibility and multi-terabit generation capability, and its low cost compared to other traffic generators. However, like most existing generators, it primarily produces constant bit rate traffic, which does not reflect the highly time-varying behavior observed in real networks, such as flashcrowds and microbursts. Such patterns are difficult to emulate at scale with current tools. We present a data plane mechanism for P4TG that shapes periodic, time-varying traffic patterns, including patterns representative of DDoS attacks and burst-load scenarios. Pattern shaping in P4TG can be applied to its generated traffic at an aggregate throughput of up to 4 Tbit/s. We evaluate pattern accuracy and analyze scalability across different sampling resolutions and periods. Further, we demonstrate practical use cases, including zero-loss throughput determination and buffer capacity measurement. Finally, we present microburst-based attack scenarios that overload UDP receivers, switch buffers, and degrade TCP throughput on shared links while remaining undetectable to conventional rate monitoring.

</details>


### [270] [A Unified Siamese Learning Framework for Zero-Day Anomaly Detection and Classification in Optical Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10827)
*Carlos Natalino,Flávia P. Monteiro,Paolo Monti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多相似度孪生神经网络，可在光网络中统一实现零日异常检测与单样本分类，无需重训练即可达到超过99%的准确率并具备即时适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光网络中新型（零日）异常层出不穷，传统方法难以快速适应未见过的异常类型且通常需要大量标注数据与频繁重训练，亟需一种能统一处理零日异常检测与单样本分类、且具备快速泛化能力的方案。

Method: 构建多相似度孪生神经网络，通过建模样本间的多种相似性关系，将零日异常检测与单样本分类统一到同一框架；利用度量学习进行表示学习，使模型在面对新光路与未见异常类型时无需重训练即可快速推理。

Result: 在不进行重训练的情况下，模型在多种光路与未见异常类型上实现超过99%的准确率，并具备即时适应能力。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了多相似度孪生网络能够有效统一光网络中的零日异常检测与单样本分类任务，兼具高精度与强泛化能力，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A multi-similarity Siamese neural network unifies zero-day anomaly detection and one-shot classification in optical networks, achieving over 99% accuracy and instant adaptability across lightpaths and unseen anomaly types without any retraining.

</details>


### [271] [Generative Explainability for Next-Generation Networks: LLM-Augmented XAI with Mutual Feature Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10942)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Sebastian Troia,Francesco Lelli,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于中等规模LLM的框架，通过结构化提示和特征交互数据生成自然语言解释，有效提升了AI/ML模型在网络运维中的可解释性，相比基线方法在有用性和范围上分别提升12.2%和6.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI/ML模型在网络运维中的广泛应用，其缺乏透明性成为运营商信任的主要障碍。现有的可解释AI（XAI）技术通常无法有效弥合这一差距，因为它们生成的技术输出难以转化为可操作的见解，特别是对非专业人员而言。

Method: 提出了一个专门设计的框架，利用中等规模的大语言模型（LLM），扩展了标准SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）特征影响值的使用。该框架采用包含互特征交互数据的结构化提示，生成人类可理解的自然语言解释。在光传输质量（QoT）估计用例上进行了实证评估，并由人类评估者进行验证。

Result: 从专家处收集的独立性能评估显示了较高的评估者间一致性。与仅使用SHAP特征影响值和简单提示的最先进基线相比，该方法在解释有用性和范围上分别提升了12.2%和6.2%，同时达到97.5%的正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了现有XAI技术在为非专业人员提供可操作见解方面的不足，通过结合LLM和增强的SHAP特征交互信息，有效提升了AI/ML模型解释的质量和实用性，增强了网络运维人员对AI系统的信任。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML) models become integral to network operations, their lack of transparency poses a significant barrier to operator trust. Existing explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) techniques often fail to bridge this gap for non-specialists, producing technical outputs that are difficult to translate into actionable insights. This paper presents a framework specifically designed to address this shortcoming. It leverages a moderately sized large language model (LLM) and extends beyond the standard use of SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) feature influence values. The framework employs a structured prompt enriched with mutual feature interaction data to generate human-understandable natural language explanations. To validate our framework, we performed an empirical evaluation on an optical quality of transmission (QoT) estimation use case with human evaluators. We collected independent performance evaluations from specialists, which showed a high inter-evaluator agreement. Compared to a state-of-the-art baseline that uses only SHAP feature influence values in a straightforward prompt, our approach improves the explanation usefulness and scope by 12.2% and 6.2%, while achieving 97.5% correctness.

</details>


### [272] [Internet Quality Barometer (IQB): A preliminary data-driven evaluation of the IQB framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11040)
*Pavlos Sermpezis,Zeynep Arslan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对互联网质量晴雨表（IQB）框架进行了初步的敏感性分析，评估了不同参数选择对IQB评分的影响，并识别了框架最敏感的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IQB框架旨在将原始互联网测量数据转化为可操作的质量洞察，通过将速度测试映射到网络需求、再映射到典型使用场景，最终聚合为0到1的单IQB评分。为了验证框架的稳健性并确保评分结果的可解释性和跨区域可比性，有必要探究不同参数选择对最终评分的影响。

Method: 作者开发了开源IQB库和原型Web应用，实现了IQB框架的大规模部署和评分计算（包括国家、地区和城市层面的全球聚合）。在此基础上，开展了初步敏感性分析，系统性考察不同参数设置对IQB评分的影响，识别高敏感参数并分析可能导致异常或误导性结果的案例。

Result: 研究识别了IQB框架中对评分结果影响最大的敏感参数，并发现了可能导致异常值或误导性结论的参数组合场景，为框架的稳健性和可靠性提供了实证依据。

Conclusion: 敏感性分析为IQB框架的参数选择和优化提供了指导，有助于提升评分结果的准确性和可信度，避免误导性结论，支持更可靠的互联网质量评估。

Abstract: The Internet Quality Barometer (IQB) framework was designed to transform raw Internet measurement data into actionable insights about Internet quality. Specifically, the framework maps raw speed test measurements to network requirements (e.g., throughput, latency), maps these requirements to representative Internet use cases (such as video streaming or web browsing), and finally aggregates performance across use cases into a single IQB score. The IQB score is a composite index ranging from 0 to 1, intended to capture overall Internet quality in a way that is both interpretable and comparable across locations. We implemented the IQB framework in practice by developing an open-source IQB library and a prototype web application. These tools enabled us to compute IQB scores at scale, including global estimates aggregated at the level of countries, regions, and cities. In this report we conduct a preliminary sensitivity analysis of the IQB framework, investigating how different parameter choices affect the resulting IQB scores, identifying which parameters the framework is most sensitive to, and highlighting cases that may lead to outliers or potentially misleading results.

</details>
